*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F_ALV
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_alv.

  DATA: i_layout TYPE lvc_s_layo,
        lwa_glay TYPE lvc_s_glay.

  DATA lt_events TYPE slis_t_event.
  DATA lt_sort TYPE lvc_t_sort.

  CONSTANTS lc_fields(11) TYPE c             VALUE 'FIELD_STYLE'.
"$$
"$$
  CONSTANTS lc_statgui TYPE slis_formname VALUE 'F_STAT_GUI'.
  CONSTANTS lc_usercom TYPE slis_formname VALUE 'F_USER_COMMAND'.

  IF sy-batch NE c_x.

*Catálogo de campos
    PERFORM f_catalogo.

*Eventos
    IF sy-batch NE c_x.

      PERFORM f_eventos
        USING lt_events.

    ENDIF.

*Sorteo de campos
    PERFORM f_sort
      USING lt_sort.

*Excluir botones.
    PERFORM f_excluding
      USING t_excluding.

    i_layout-stylefname = lc_fields.
    i_layout-zebra = c_x.
    i_layout-cwidth_opt = c_x.

    IF sy-batch NE c_x.

      lwa_glay-edt_cll_cb = c_x.

    ENDIF.
"$$
"$$
"$$

"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
    CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_GRID_DISPLAY_LVC'
      EXPORTING
        i_callback_program       = sy-cprog
        is_layout_lvc            = i_layout
        i_callback_pf_status_set = lc_statgui
        i_callback_user_command  = lc_usercom
        i_grid_settings          = lwa_glay
        it_fieldcat_lvc          = t_fieldcat
        it_excluding             = t_excluding
        it_sort_lvc              = lt_sort
        i_save                   = c_x
        it_events                = lt_events
"$$
      TABLES
        t_outtab                 = t_zta1_aux
      EXCEPTIONS
        program_error            = 1
        OTHERS                   = 2.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0.

      MESSAGE ID sy-msgid
            TYPE sy-msgty
          NUMBER sy-msgno
            WITH sy-msgv1
                 sy-msgv2
                 sy-msgv3
                 sy-msgv4.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F_CATALOGO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_catalogo .

  DATA: lwa_fieldcat TYPE lvc_s_fcat.

  CONSTANTS lc_fp(11) TYPE c VALUE 'ZTFORMAPAGO'.
  CONSTANTS lc_zta TYPE dd02l-tabname VALUE 'ZTA1_CP1'.
  CONSTANTS lc_zta2 TYPE dd02l-tabname VALUE 'ZTA2_CP2'.

  CLEAR: lwa_fieldcat,
         t_fieldcat[].

  CALL FUNCTION 'LVC_FIELDCATALOG_MERGE'
    EXPORTING
*     i_structure_name       = lc_zta--------check
      i_structure_name       = lc_zta
"$$
    CHANGING
      ct_fieldcat            = t_fieldcat
    EXCEPTIONS
      inconsistent_interface = 1
      program_error          = 2
      OTHERS                 = 3.

  IF sy-subrc <> 0.

    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid
          TYPE sy-msgty
        NUMBER sy-msgno
          WITH sy-msgv1
               sy-msgv2
               sy-msgv3
               sy-msgv4.

  ELSE.

    LOOP AT t_fieldcat
       INTO lwa_fieldcat.

"$$
"$$
      IF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_seleccion.

        lwa_fieldcat-edit = c_x.
        lwa_fieldcat-checkbox = c_x.

      ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_uuid_fac.

        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_l = TEXT-c27.
        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_m = TEXT-c27.
        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_s = TEXT-c27.
        lwa_fieldcat-seltext   = TEXT-c27.
        lwa_fieldcat-edit = c_x.

      ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_uuid_comp.

        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_l = TEXT-c28.
        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_m = TEXT-c28.
        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_s = TEXT-c28.
        lwa_fieldcat-seltext   = TEXT-c28.

      ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_estatus.

        lwa_fieldcat-icon      = c_x.

      ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_forma_pag.

        lwa_fieldcat-edit       = c_x.
        lwa_fieldcat-f4availabl = c_x.
        lwa_fieldcat-ref_field  = lc_fp.
        lwa_fieldcat-ref_table  = lc_zta.

      ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_rfc_ord.

        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_l = TEXT-c23.
        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_m = TEXT-c23.
        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_s = TEXT-c23.
        lwa_fieldcat-seltext   = TEXT-c23.
        lwa_fieldcat-edit      = c_x.

      ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_rfc_ben.

        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_l = TEXT-c24.
        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_m = TEXT-c24.
        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_s = TEXT-c24.
        lwa_fieldcat-seltext   = TEXT-c24.
        lwa_fieldcat-edit = c_x."------check

      ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_anio_pago.

        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_l = TEXT-c25.
        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_m = TEXT-c25.
        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_s = TEXT-c25.
        lwa_fieldcat-seltext   = TEXT-c25.

      ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_anio_fac.

        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_l = TEXT-c26.
        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_m = TEXT-c26.
        lwa_fieldcat-scrtext_s = TEXT-c26.
        lwa_fieldcat-seltext   = TEXT-c26.

      ELSEIF
              lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_salant OR
*             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_num_par OR      "WLECEA
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_zsust OR         "WLECEA
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_imp_pag OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_cta_benef OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_nom_ban OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_cta_ord OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_mpag OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_no_oper OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_folio_fac.

        lwa_fieldcat-edit = c_x.

      ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_tipo_cadpago OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_num_par OR      "WLECEA
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_cert_pago OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_nocert OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_cad_pago OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_sello_pag OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_serie OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_rutaxml OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_rutapdf OR
             lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_num_par.  "VYGC

        lwa_fieldcat-no_out = c_x.

      ENDIF.

      MODIFY t_fieldcat
        FROM lwa_fieldcat
       INDEX sy-tabix.

    ENDLOOP.

    PERFORM f_catalogo_impuestos.  "* Complemento de Pago 2.0
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$

"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
"$$
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F_MOD_SCREEN

*& Modificar campos en pantalla
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_mod_screen .

  IF "screen-name = '%_P_DOCANU_%_APP_%-TEXT'      OR
     "screen-name = 'P_DOCANU'                     OR
     screen-name = '%_S_DOCPAG_%_APP_%-VALU_PUSH' OR
     screen-name = 'S_DOCPAG-LOW'                 OR
     screen-name = 'S_DOCPAG-HIGH'                OR
     screen-name = '%_S_DOCPAG_%_APP_%-TEXT'      OR
     screen-name = '%_S_DOCANU_%_APP_%-VALU_PUSH' OR
     screen-name = 'P_PIND'                       OR
     screen-name = '%_P_PIND_%_APP_%-TEXT'        OR
     screen-name = 'P_PMAS'                       OR
     screen-name = '%_P_PMAS_%_APP_%-TEXT'        OR
     screen-name = 'P_TIMB'                       OR
     screen-name = '%_P_TIMB_%_APP_%-TEXT'        OR
     screen-name = 'P_CERR'                       OR
     screen-name = '%_P_CERR_%_APP_%-TEXT'        OR
     screen-name = 'P_UUIDPA'                     OR
     screen-name = '%_P_UUIDPA_%_APP_%-TEXT'      OR
     screen-name = '%_S_DOCANU_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH' OR
     screen-name = 'S_DOCANU-LOW'                 OR
     screen-name = 'S_DOCANU-HIGH'                OR
     screen-name = '%_S_DOCANU_%_APP_%-TEXT'      OR
     screen-name = '%_S_PAGIND_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH' OR
     screen-name = 'S_PAGIND-LOW'                 OR
     screen-name = 'S_PAGIND-HIGH'                OR
     screen-name = '%_S_PAGIND_%_APP_%-TEXT'      OR
     screen-name = '%_S_BUKRS_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
     screen-name = 'S_BUKRS-LOW'                  OR
     screen-name = 'S_BUKRS-HIGH'                 OR
     screen-name = '%_S_BUKRS_%_APP_%-TEXT'       OR
     screen-name = '%_S_MONAT_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
     screen-name = 'S_MONAT-LOW'                  OR
     screen-name = 'S_MONAT-HIGH'                 OR
     screen-name = '%_S_MONAT_%_APP_%-TEXT'       OR
     screen-name = '%_S_GJAHR_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
     screen-name = 'S_GJAHR-LOW'                  OR
     screen-name = 'S_GJAHR-HIGH'                 OR
     screen-name = '%_S_GJAHR_%_APP_%-TEXT'       OR
     screen-name = '%_S_KUNNR_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
     screen-name = 'S_KUNNR-LOW'                  OR
     screen-name = 'S_KUNNR-HIGH'                 OR
     screen-name = '%_S_KUNNR_%_APP_%-TEXT'       OR
     screen-name = '%_S_GJAHRA_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH' OR
     screen-name = 'S_GJAHRA-LOW'                 OR
     screen-name = 'S_GJAHRA-HIGH'                OR
     screen-name = '%_S_GJAHRA_%_APP_%-TEXT'      OR
     screen-name = '%_S_GJAHRS_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH' OR
     screen-name = 'S_GJAHRS-LOW'                 OR
     screen-name = 'S_GJAHRS-HIGH'                OR
     screen-name = '%_S_GJAHRS_%_APP_%-TEXT'.

    screen-input = 0.

  ENDIF.

*Emisión
  IF p_emit = c_x.

    IF   screen-name = 'P_PIND'                       OR
         screen-name = '%_P_PIND_%_APP_%-TEXT'        OR
         screen-name = 'P_PMAS'                       OR
         screen-name = '%_P_PMAS_%_APP_%-TEXT'       OR
         screen-name = '%_S_BUKRS_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
         screen-name = 'S_BUKRS-LOW'                  OR
         screen-name = 'S_BUKRS-HIGH'                 OR
         screen-name = '%_S_BUKRS_%_APP_%-TEXT'       OR
         screen-name = '%_S_GJAHR_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
         screen-name = 'S_GJAHR-LOW'                  OR
         screen-name = 'S_GJAHR-HIGH'                 OR
         screen-name = '%_S_GJAHR_%_APP_%-TEXT'."       OR
*         screen-name = '%_S_KUNNR_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
*         screen-name = 'S_KUNNR-LOW'                  OR
*         screen-name = 'S_KUNNR-HIGH'                 OR
*         screen-name = '%_S_KUNNR_%_APP_%-TEXT'    .

      screen-input = 1.

    ENDIF.

    IF screen-name = '%_S_KUNNR_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
       screen-name = 'S_KUNNR-LOW'                  OR
       screen-name = 'S_KUNNR-HIGH'                 OR
       screen-name = '%_S_KUNNR_%_APP_%-TEXT'    .

      IF p_pind = c_x.

        REFRESH s_kunnr[].

        screen-input = 0.

      ELSEIF p_pmas = c_x.

        screen-input = 1.

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

    IF screen-name = '%_S_PAGIND_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH' OR
       screen-name = 'S_PAGIND-LOW'                 OR
       screen-name = 'S_PAGIND-HIGH'                OR
       screen-name = '%_S_PAGIND_%_APP_%-TEXT'    .

      IF p_pind = c_x.

        screen-input = 1.

      ELSEIF p_pmas = c_x.

        REFRESH s_pagind[].

        screen-input = 0.

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

    IF screen-name = '%_S_MONAT_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
       screen-name = 'S_MONAT-LOW'                  OR
       screen-name = 'S_MONAT-HIGH'                 OR
       screen-name = '%_S_MONAT_%_APP_%-TEXT'.

      IF p_pind = c_x.

        CLEAR: s_monat[].
        CLEAR: s_monat.
        REFRESH: s_monat.

        screen-input = 0.

      ELSEIF p_pmas = c_x.

        screen-input = 1.

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

*Sustituir
  IF p_sust = c_x.

    IF "screen-name = '%_P_DOCANU_%_APP_%-TEXT'      OR
       "screen-name = 'P_DOCANU'                     OR
       screen-name = 'P_UUIDPA'                     OR
       screen-name = '%_P_UUIDPA_%_APP_%-TEXT'      OR
       screen-name = '%_S_DOCANU_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH' OR
       screen-name = 'S_DOCANU-LOW'                 OR
       screen-name = 'S_DOCANU-HIGH'                OR
       screen-name = '%_S_DOCANU_%_APP_%-TEXT'      OR
       screen-name = '%_S_DOCANU_%_APP_%-VALU_PUSH' OR
       screen-name = '%_S_DOCPAG_%_APP_%-VALU_PUSH' OR
       screen-name = 'S_DOCPAG-LOW'                 OR
       screen-name = 'S_DOCPAG-HIGH'                OR
       screen-name = '%_S_DOCPAG_%_APP_%-TEXT'      OR
       screen-name = '%_S_BUKRS_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
       screen-name = 'S_BUKRS-LOW'                  OR
       screen-name = 'S_BUKRS-HIGH'                 OR
       screen-name = '%_S_BUKRS_%_APP_%-TEXT'       OR
       screen-name = '%_S_GJAHRA_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH' OR
       screen-name = 'S_GJAHRA-LOW'                 OR
       screen-name = 'S_GJAHRA-HIGH'                OR
       screen-name = '%_S_GJAHRA_%_APP_%-TEXT'      OR
       screen-name = '%_S_GJAHRS_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH' OR
       screen-name = 'S_GJAHRS-LOW'                 OR
       screen-name = 'S_GJAHRS-HIGH'                OR
       screen-name = '%_S_GJAHRS_%_APP_%-TEXT'.

      REFRESH: s_monat[],
               s_kunnr[],
               s_gjahr[].

      screen-input = 1.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

*Reporte
  IF p_repo = c_x.

    IF screen-name = 'P_TIMB'                       OR
       screen-name = '%_P_TIMB_%_APP_%-TEXT'        OR
       screen-name = 'P_CERR'                       OR
       screen-name = '%_P_CERR_%_APP_%-TEXT'        OR
       screen-name = '%_S_BUKRS_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
       screen-name = 'S_BUKRS-LOW'                  OR
       screen-name = 'S_BUKRS-HIGH'                 OR
       screen-name = '%_S_BUKRS_%_APP_%-TEXT'       OR
       screen-name = '%_S_GJAHR_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
       screen-name = 'S_GJAHR-LOW'                  OR
       screen-name = 'S_GJAHR-HIGH'                 OR
       screen-name = '%_S_GJAHR_%_APP_%-TEXT'       OR
       screen-name = '%_S_KUNNR_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
       screen-name = 'S_KUNNR-LOW'                  OR
       screen-name = 'S_KUNNR-HIGH'                 OR
       screen-name = '%_S_KUNNR_%_APP_%-TEXT'    .

      REFRESH: s_monat[].

      screen-input = 1.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

  IF p_renv = c_x.

    IF screen-name = '%_S_BUKRS_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
       screen-name = 'S_BUKRS-LOW'                  OR
       screen-name = 'S_BUKRS-HIGH'                 OR
       screen-name = '%_S_BUKRS_%_APP_%-TEXT'       OR
       screen-name = '%_S_GJAHR_%_APP_%-OPTI_PUSH'  OR
       screen-name = 'S_GJAHR-LOW'                  OR
       screen-name = 'S_GJAHR-HIGH'                 OR
       screen-name = '%_S_GJAHR_%_APP_%-TEXT'.

      REFRESH: s_monat[].
      REFRESH: s_kunnr[].

      screen-input = 1.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

FORM f_stat_gui

  SET PF-STATUS 'STANDARD' EXCLUDING t_excluding. "Nombre del Status GUI

ENDFORM.
FORM alv_force_refresh.
  DATA: lo_grid   TYPE REF TO cl_gui_alv_grid,
        ls_stable TYPE lvc_s_stbl.

  ls_stable-row = 'X'.
  ls_stable-col = 'X'.

  CALL FUNCTION 'GET_GLOBALS_FROM_SLVC_FULLSCR'
    IMPORTING e_grid = lo_grid
    EXCEPTIONS OTHERS = 1.

  IF lo_grid IS BOUND.
    lo_grid->refresh_table_display(
      EXPORTING
        is_stable      = ls_stable
        i_soft_refresh = abap_true ).
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.

FORM f_user_command USING r_ucomm     LIKE sy-ucomm
                          rs_selfield TYPE slis_selfield.

  DATA: lwa_z TYPE zsa1_cp1.

  " Siempre mantener estable el scroll del ALV
  rs_selfield-refresh    = 'X'.
  rs_selfield-col_stable = 'X'.
  rs_selfield-row_stable = 'X'.

  " BACK/EXIT/CANCEL del ALV
  IF r_ucomm = '&F03' OR r_ucomm = '&F12' OR r_ucomm = '&F15'
   OR r_ucomm = 'BACK' OR r_ucomm = 'EXIT' OR r_ucomm = 'CANC'.
    gv_alv_user_exit = abap_true.
    rs_selfield-exit = 'X'.
    CLEAR r_ucomm.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  CASE r_ucomm.
    WHEN 'EMIT'.  " <- tu botón de emitir en el ALV
      " Validar que haya filas y selección
      IF t_zta1_2 IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE 'No hay registros en el ALV' TYPE 'I'.
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.
       READ TABLE t_zta1_2 WITH KEY seleccion = 'X' TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE 'Seleccione al menos un registro' TYPE 'I'.
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.

      " Ejecutar emisión
      PERFORM f_emitir_boton.
   WHEN 'SELECT'.
      LOOP AT t_zta1_2 ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<r>) WHERE estatus NE c_rojo.
        <r>-seleccion = 'X'.
      ENDLOOP.

    WHEN 'NOSELECT'.
      LOOP AT t_zta1_2 ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<r2>) WHERE estatus NE c_rojo.
        CLEAR <r2>-seleccion.
      ENDLOOP.

    WHEN OTHERS.
      " aquí quedan tus otros comandos VIS_XML, VIS_PDF, CANCEL, etc.
      " (se mantienen sin cambios en tu include de lógica)
  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.



*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F_TBL_BANCOS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Tabla de mantenimiento bancos
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_tbl_bancos .

  AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT 'S_TCODE'
                      ID 'TCD'
                   FIELD 'ZFI_TBANCOS'.

  IF sy-subrc = 0.

    CALL TRANSACTION 'ZFI_TBANCOS'.

  ELSE.

    MESSAGE TEXT-003
       TYPE c_s
    DISPLAY LIKE c_e.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

FORM f_eventos  USING lt_events TYPE slis_t_event.
  DATA: l_event TYPE slis_alv_event.

  REFRESH lt_events.

  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_EVENTS_GET'
    EXPORTING
      i_list_type = 0
    IMPORTING
      et_events   = lt_events.

  " DATA_CHANGED (para toggle de la casilla SELECCION y demás reglas)
  l_event-name = 'DATA_CHANGED'.
  l_event-form = 'DATA_CHANGED'.
  APPEND l_event TO lt_events.
ENDFORM.
FORM data_changed  USING rr_data TYPE REF TO cl_alv_changed_data_protocol.

  DATA: lw_mod     TYPE lvc_s_modi,
        lw_ztfpago TYPE ztfpago,
        lwa_aux    TYPE zsa1_cp1,
        lv_cont    TYPE i.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <row> TYPE zsa1_cp1.

  " --- 1) Cambios de mt_mod_cells ---
  LOOP AT rr_data->mt_mod_cells INTO lw_mod.

    " Toggle de la casilla SELECCION en t_zta1_2
    DATA lv_fname TYPE lvc_fname.
    lv_fname = 'SELECCION'.



    IF lw_mod-fieldname = c_seleccion.
      ASSIGN t_zta1_2[ lw_mod-row_id ] TO <row>.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        <row>-seleccion = xsdbool( lw_mod-value = 'X' OR lw_mod-value = '1' ).
       rr_data->modify_cell( i_row_id = lw_mod-row_id i_fieldname = lv_fname i_value = <row>-seleccion ).
      ENDIF.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

    " === A partir de aquí conserva tu lógica original de validación ===
    IF lw_mod-fieldname = c_forma_pag AND lw_mod-value IS NOT INITIAL.
      READ TABLE t_ztfpago INTO lw_ztfpago
           WITH KEY fpago = lw_mod-value BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        MESSAGE TEXT-007 TYPE 'E'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    " ... resto de tu lógica existente (imp_pag, salant, mpag, etc.) ...

  ENDLOOP.

  " --- 2) (Opcional) también soporta mt_good_cells si tu sistema lo usa ---
  LOOP AT rr_data->mt_good_cells INTO lw_mod.
    IF lw_mod-fieldname = c_seleccion.
      ASSIGN t_zta1_2[ lw_mod-row_id ] TO <row>.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        <row>-seleccion = xsdbool( lw_mod-value = 'X' OR lw_mod-value = '1' ).
       rr_data->modify_cell( i_row_id = lw_mod-row_id i_fieldname = lv_fname i_value = <row>-seleccion ).
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.


*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_VERIFICAR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Funcionalidad de boton Verificar
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*



"======================
" Verificación (ahora con USING iv_from_back)
"======================

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  f_verificar
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&  Muestra/verifica y arma datos para ALV. Si venimos de BACK, sale.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_verificar USING iv_from_back TYPE abap_bool.

  " Si ya sabemos que venimos de BACK (del ALV) o nos lo pasan explícito,
  " NO valides nada y sal de inmediato.
  IF iv_from_back = abap_true OR gv_from_back = abap_true.
    CLEAR: gv_from_back.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  " 1) Validaciones y preparación de registros seleccionados
  PERFORM f_valida_registros USING abap_false.  "<<< ahora con USING

  " 2) Si aquí activaste mensajes y no hay nada que mostrar, puedes salir
  IF t_zta1_2 IS INITIAL AND t_zta1_aux IS INITIAL.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  " 3) Mostrar ALV de emisión (usa layout edit y eventos)
  PERFORM f_alv_el USING 'F_STAT_GUI_EMITIR'.

  " 4) Si el usuario salió del ALV con F3/F12, marca para cortar arriba
  IF gv_alv_user_exit = abap_true.
    gv_from_back = abap_true.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.


*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  f_valida_registros
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_valida_registros USING iv_from_back TYPE abap_bool.

  " Si venimos de BACK, NO valides nada
  IF iv_from_back = abap_true OR gv_from_back = abap_true.
    CLEAR gv_from_back.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  DATA lw_zta1_aux TYPE zsa1_cp1.
  DATA lwa_ztmpago TYPE ztmpago.

  CONSTANTS: lc_ord(13) TYPE c VALUE 'XEXX010101000',
             lc_pip(3)  TYPE c VALUE 'PIP',
             lc_ppd(3)  TYPE c VALUE 'PPD'.

  DATA lv_tabix TYPE sy-tabix.
  DATA lv_pago  TYPE zta1_cp1-ztpago.
  DATA lv_fpago TYPE zta1_cp1-ztformapago.
  DATA lv_noper TYPE zta1_cp1-no_oper.

  REFRESH t_zta1_2[].

  LOOP AT t_zta1_aux INTO lw_zta1_aux WHERE seleccion = c_x.

    IF lw_zta1_aux-estatus = c_rojo.
      MESSAGE TEXT-183 TYPE c_e.
    ELSE.

      lv_tabix = lv_tabix + 1.
      IF lv_tabix = 1.
        lv_pago  = lw_zta1_aux-ztpago.
        lv_fpago = lw_zta1_aux-ztformapago.
        lv_noper = lw_zta1_aux-no_oper.
      ENDIF.

      "=== (aquí deja TODAS TUS VALIDACIONES sin cambio) ==================
      "  - forma de pago homogénea por pago
      "  - no_oper homogéneo
      "  - met_pagodr requerido y válido contra t_ztmpago
      "  - ztformapago requerido
      "  - moneda ≠ MXN -> tcambio, tc_dr, imp_pag, etc.
      "  - RFC ordenante extranjero -> banco ordenante requerido
      "  - tipo_cadpago → cert/cad/sello
      "  - PIP/PPD → num_par, imp_sal_ant, imp_sal_ins >= 0
      "  - al final APPEND a t_zta1_2
      "====================================================================

      " Ejemplos (idénticos a los tuyos):
      IF lv_pago = lw_zta1_aux-ztpago.
        IF lv_fpago NE lw_zta1_aux-ztformapago.
          MESSAGE TEXT-201 TYPE c_e.
        ENDIF.
        IF lv_noper NE lw_zta1_aux-no_oper.
          MESSAGE TEXT-202 TYPE c_e.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        lv_fpago = lw_zta1_aux-ztformapago.
        lv_noper = lw_zta1_aux-no_oper.
      ENDIF.

      IF lw_zta1_aux-met_pagodr IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE TEXT-184 TYPE c_e.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE t_ztmpago INTO lwa_ztmpago
             WITH KEY mpago = lw_zta1_aux-met_pagodr
             BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          MESSAGE TEXT-200 TYPE c_e.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      IF lw_zta1_aux-ztformapago IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE TEXT-006 TYPE c_e.
      ENDIF.

      IF lw_zta1_aux-ztmonp <> c_mxn.
        IF lw_zta1_aux-met_pagodr = lc_pip OR lw_zta1_aux-met_pagodr = lc_ppd.
          IF lw_zta1_aux-tcambio IS INITIAL.
            MESSAGE TEXT-012 TYPE c_e.
          ELSEIF lw_zta1_aux-tc_dr IS INITIAL.
            MESSAGE TEXT-017 TYPE c_e.
          ELSEIF lw_zta1_aux-tc_dr IS NOT INITIAL AND lw_zta1_aux-imp_pag IS INITIAL.
            MESSAGE TEXT-020 TYPE c_e.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      IF lw_zta1_aux-rfc_ord = lc_ord.
        IF lw_zta1_aux-ztbcoord IS INITIAL.
          MESSAGE TEXT-013 TYPE c_e.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      IF lw_zta1_aux-tipo_cadpago <> '' AND lw_zta1_aux-ztformapago = 03.
        IF lw_zta1_aux-cert_pago IS INITIAL.
          MESSAGE TEXT-014 TYPE c_e.
        ELSEIF lw_zta1_aux-cad_pago IS INITIAL.
          MESSAGE TEXT-015 TYPE c_e.
        ELSEIF lw_zta1_aux-sello_pag IS INITIAL.
          MESSAGE TEXT-016 TYPE c_e.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      IF lw_zta1_aux-met_pagodr = lc_pip OR lw_zta1_aux-met_pagodr = lc_ppd.
        IF lw_zta1_aux-num_par IS INITIAL.
          MESSAGE TEXT-018 TYPE c_e.
        ELSEIF lw_zta1_aux-imp_sal_ant IS INITIAL.
          MESSAGE TEXT-019 TYPE c_e.
        ELSEIF lw_zta1_aux-imp_sal_ins < 0.
          MESSAGE TEXT-203 TYPE c_e.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      APPEND lw_zta1_aux TO t_zta1_2.
      lv_pago = lw_zta1_aux-ztpago.
      CLEAR lw_zta1_aux.

    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

  gv_case_cp20 = 'TOTG'.
  PERFORM ajustes_complemento_20.

ENDFORM.
FORM f_alv_el  USING  lv_stat_gui TYPE slis_formname.

  DATA: lwa_layout TYPE slis_layout_alv,
        lt_events  TYPE slis_t_event.

  CONSTANTS lc_usercom TYPE slis_formname VALUE 'F_USER_COMMAND'.

  " Layout
  CLEAR lwa_layout.
  lwa_layout-zebra             = 'X'.
  lwa_layout-colwidth_optimize = 'X'.

  " Catálogo y botones a excluir
  PERFORM f_catalogo_emitir.
  PERFORM f_excluding USING t_excluding.

  " Eventos (DATA_CHANGED)
  PERFORM f_eventos USING lt_events.

  CLEAR gv_alv_user_exit.

  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_GRID_DISPLAY'
    EXPORTING
      i_callback_program        = sy-repid
      i_callback_pf_status_set  = lv_stat_gui        " 'F_STAT_GUI_EMITIR' ó 'F_STAT_GUI_LOG'
      i_callback_user_command   = lc_usercom
      is_layout                 = lwa_layout
      it_fieldcat               = t_fieldcat_emitir
      it_excluding              = t_excluding
      it_events                 = lt_events
    TABLES
      t_outtab                  = t_zta1_2
    EXCEPTIONS
      program_error             = 1
      OTHERS                    = 2.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
       WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.


*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_CATALOGO_EMITIR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*


*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_CATALOGO_EMITIR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Estauts gui pantalla de emitir
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_catalogo_emitir .

  DATA: lwa_fieldcat TYPE slis_fieldcat_alv,
        lv_struct    TYPE dd02l-tabname VALUE 'ZTA1_CP1'. "estructura base

  REFRESH t_fieldcat_emitir[].

  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_FIELDCATALOG_MERGE'
    EXPORTING
      i_program_name   = sy-repid
      i_structure_name = lv_struct
    CHANGING
      ct_fieldcat      = t_fieldcat_emitir
    EXCEPTIONS
      OTHERS           = 1.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
       WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT t_fieldcat_emitir INTO lwa_fieldcat.

    " Casilla de selección editable
    IF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_seleccion.
      lwa_fieldcat-checkbox  = 'X'.
      lwa_fieldcat-edit      = 'X'.
      lwa_fieldcat-outputlen = 1.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_s = 'Sel.'.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_m = 'Sel.'.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_l = 'Seleccion'.
      MODIFY t_fieldcat_emitir FROM lwa_fieldcat.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

    " Etiquetas conocidas (si existen en tu estructura)
    IF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_uuid_fac.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_l    = TEXT-c27.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_m    = TEXT-c27.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_s    = TEXT-c27.
      lwa_fieldcat-reptext_ddic = TEXT-c27.
    ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_uuid_comp.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_l    = TEXT-c28.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_m    = TEXT-c28.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_s    = TEXT-c28.
      lwa_fieldcat-reptext_ddic = TEXT-c28.
    ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_rfc_ord.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_l    = TEXT-c23.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_m    = TEXT-c23.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_s    = TEXT-c23.
      lwa_fieldcat-reptext_ddic = TEXT-c23.
    ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_rfc_ben.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_l    = TEXT-c24.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_m    = TEXT-c24.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_s    = TEXT-c24.
      lwa_fieldcat-reptext_ddic = TEXT-c24.
    ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_anio_pago.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_l    = TEXT-c25.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_m    = TEXT-c25.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_s    = TEXT-c25.
      lwa_fieldcat-reptext_ddic = TEXT-c25.
    ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_anio_fac.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_l    = TEXT-c26.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_m    = TEXT-c26.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_s    = TEXT-c26.
      lwa_fieldcat-reptext_ddic = TEXT-c26.
    ELSEIF lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = c_numcomp.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_l    = TEXT-c29.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_m    = TEXT-c29.
      lwa_fieldcat-seltext_s    = TEXT-c29.
      lwa_fieldcat-reptext_ddic = TEXT-c29.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY t_fieldcat_emitir FROM lwa_fieldcat.

  ENDLOOP.

  " Si tienes subrutina de impuestos, mantenla
  PERFORM f_catalogo_impuestos_emitir.

ENDFORM.






*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_EMITIR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Proceso para el boton emitir
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_emitir .

  CONSTANTS lc_gui_log(30) TYPE c VALUE 'F_STAT_GUI_LOG'.
  CONSTANTS lc_1 TYPE c VALUE '1'.
  CONSTANTS lc_cliente(2) TYPE c VALUE 'CL'.
  CONSTANTS lc_rfc(3) TYPE c VALUE 'RFC'.
  CONSTANTS lc_pue(3) TYPE c VALUE 'PUE'.
  CONSTANTS lc_etxt(4) TYPE c VALUE '.txt'.
  CONSTANTS lc_exml(4) TYPE c VALUE '.XML'.
  CONSTANTS lc_epdf(4) TYPE c VALUE '.PDF'.
  CONSTANTS: c_log_objt   TYPE balobj_d    VALUE 'ZCP',
             c_log_subobj TYPE balsubobj   VALUE 'PROCESS'.
  DATA lv_answer TYPE c.
  DATA lv_ruta_xml TYPE string.
  DATA lv_ruta_pdf TYPE string.
  DATA lv_factura TYPE vbrk-vbeln.
  DATA lv_text_stat TYPE string.
  DATA lv_stat TYPE zta1_cp1-estatus.
  DATA lv_nom_pdf TYPE so_obj_des.
  DATA lv_nom_xml TYPE so_obj_des.
  DATA lv_name TYPE string.
  DATA lv_name2 TYPE string.
  DATA lv_ext TYPE string.

  DATA lv_sello TYPE string.
  DATA lv_cert TYPE string.
  DATA lv_nocert TYPE string.
  DATA v_key_1 TYPE char35.
  DATA v_key_2 TYPE char35.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_key_1>.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fs_key_2>.

  DATA lwa_clientes TYPE zsa1_cp1. "ty_zta1_cp1.
  DATA lwa_factura_010 TYPE ty_factura_010.
  DATA lwa_factura_012 TYPE ty_factura_012.
  DATA lwa_factura_017 TYPE ty_factura_017.
  DATA lwa_vbrk TYPE ty_vbrk.
  DATA lwa_t001 TYPE ty_t001.
  DATA lwa_t880 TYPE ty_t880.
  DATA lwa_t005 TYPE ty_t005.
  DATA lwa_t001z TYPE ty_t001z.
  DATA lwa_kna1 TYPE ty_kna1.
  DATA lwa_zta1_cp1 TYPE zta1_cp1.
  DATA lwa_zta1_2 TYPE zsa1_cp1.
  DATA lwa_zta1_3 TYPE zsa1_cp1.
  DATA lwa_bkpf_sd TYPE ty_bkpf_sd.
  DATA lwa_adrc TYPE ty_adrc.
  DATA lwa_datos_mail TYPE ty_datos_mail.
  DATA lwa_saldos TYPE ztssaldos.
  DATA lwa_zta1_cp1_ren TYPE zta1_cp1. "PRENV
*  DATA lwa_lugexp TYPE zta1_lugexp.

  DATA lt_clientes TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zsa1_cp1. "ty_zta1_cp1.
  DATA lt_pagos TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zsa1_cp1. "ty_zta1_cp1.
  DATA lt_fac TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zsa1_cp1. "ty_zta1_cp1.
  DATA lt_pdf_tab TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF tline.
  DATA lt_saldos TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ztssaldos.

  DATA lv_size_pdf TYPE so_obj_len.
  DATA lv_pdf_xstring TYPE xstring.
  DATA lv_xml_string TYPE stringval. "string.

  DATA: i_zta1_cp1 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zta1_cp1,
        lv_rfc     TYPE stcd1,
        lv_ztpago  TYPE zta1_cp1-ztpago.
  DATA: lc_regimen601(03) TYPE c VALUE '601'.  "CP 2.0 dnavoa 22.07.22 (El regimenFiscal se maneja por codigo)
  DATA :
    lo_logger   TYPE REF TO zcl_logger, " Mensajes
    lt_messages TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapiret1.

  DATA lo TYPE REF TO zcl_cpago_xml_builder_cp20.
  DATA:
    ty_t_bapiret1 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapiret1 WITH EMPTY KEY,
    lt_msgs       TYPE zcl_cpago_xml_builder_cp20=>ty_t_bapiret1.
*  DATA lt_datos_mail TYPE zcl_cpago_xml_builder_cp20=>ty_tt_datos_mail.

  IF lo IS INITIAL.
    CREATE OBJECT lo.
  ENDIF.

  IF sy-batch = c_x.

    lv_answer = lc_1.

  ELSE.

*POPUP
    CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
      EXPORTING
        titlebar       = TEXT-s01 "lc_conf
        text_question  = TEXT-s02 "lc_question
        text_button_1  = TEXT-s03 "lc_si
        text_button_2  = TEXT-s04 "lc_no
      IMPORTING
        answer         = lv_answer
      EXCEPTIONS
        text_not_found = 1
        OTHERS         = 2.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0.

      MESSAGE ID sy-msgid
            TYPE sy-msgty
          NUMBER sy-msgno
            WITH sy-msgv1
                 sy-msgv2
                 sy-msgv3
                 sy-msgv4.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.


  DATA ipaso TYPE i.

  IF lv_answer = lc_1.


*  BEGIN OF MOD WLECEA.
    CLEAR: lv_rfc.
"$$
    t_zta1_2[] = t_zta1_aux[].
    t_zta1_3[] = t_zta1_aux[].
    "RECUPERO SOLO LOS NUMEROS DE COBRO
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM t_zta1_3
                         COMPARING
                                   ztpago.

    LOOP AT t_zta1_3 INTO lwa_zta1_3 WHERE seleccion = 'X'.
      CLEAR :
            lv_ztpago,
            v_cons.
      t_zta1_2[] = t_zta1_aux[].
      DELETE t_zta1_2 WHERE ztpago NE lwa_zta1_3-ztpago.

      LOOP AT t_zta1_2 INTO lwa_zta1_2 WHERE seleccion = 'X' AND ztpago = lwa_zta1_3-ztpago.
*         loop at T_ZTA1_AUX INTO lwa_zta1_2.
*          IF lwa_zta1_2-numcomp IS INITIAL. "VYGC
*          IF lwa_zta1_2-rfc_cl NE lv_rfc.

        "moneda_dr
        IF lwa_zta1_2-ztmonp = 'MXN'.
          lv_decimales = 2.
        ELSE.
          lv_decimales = 6.
        ENDIF.



        IF lwa_zta1_2-ztpago NE lv_ztpago.
          CALL FUNCTION 'NUMBER_GET_NEXT'
            EXPORTING
              nr_range_nr             = '01'
              object                  = 'ZFI_COMP'
            IMPORTING
              number                  = v_cons
            EXCEPTIONS
              interval_not_found      = 1
              number_range_not_intern = 2
              object_not_found        = 3
              quantity_is_0           = 4
              quantity_is_not_1       = 5
              interval_overflow       = 6
              buffer_overflow         = 7
              OTHERS                  = 8.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.

            IF ipaso = 0.
              CREATE OBJECT lo_logger
                EXPORTING
                  iv_extnumber = v_cons
                  iv_object    = c_log_objt
                  iv_subobject = c_log_subobj
                  iv_repid     = sy-repid.
              IF sy-subrc = 0.
                ipaso = 1.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
*     Begin of

        DATA: lv_tottrlbaseiva16  TYPE  ztottrlbase,
              lv_tottrlimptoiva16 TYPE  ztottrlimpto,
              lv_tottrlbaseiva8   TYPE  ztottrlbase,
              lv_tottrlimptoiva8  TYPE  ztottrlimpto.

*     Total IVA 16%

        IF NOT lwa_zta1_2-tottrlbaseiva16 IS INITIAL
          AND lv_tottrlbaseiva16 IS INITIAL.

          lv_tottrlbaseiva16 = lwa_zta1_2-tottrlbaseiva16.

        ENDIF.

        IF NOT lwa_zta1_2-tottrlimptoiva16 IS INITIAL
          AND lv_tottrlimptoiva16 IS INITIAL.

          lv_tottrlimptoiva16 = lwa_zta1_2-tottrlimptoiva16.

        ENDIF.

        IF lwa_zta1_2-tottrlbaseiva16 IS INITIAL
          AND NOT lv_tottrlbaseiva16 IS INITIAL.

          lwa_zta1_2-tottrlbaseiva16 = lv_tottrlbaseiva16.

        ENDIF.

        IF lwa_zta1_2-tottrlimptoiva16 IS INITIAL
          AND NOT lv_tottrlimptoiva16 IS INITIAL.

          lwa_zta1_2-tottrlimptoiva16 = lv_tottrlimptoiva16.

        ENDIF.

*     Total IVA 8%

        IF NOT lwa_zta1_2-tottrlbaseiva8 IS INITIAL
          AND lv_tottrlbaseiva8 IS INITIAL.

          lv_tottrlbaseiva8 = lwa_zta1_2-tottrlbaseiva8.

        ENDIF.

        IF NOT lwa_zta1_2-tottrlimptoiva8 IS INITIAL
          AND lv_tottrlimptoiva8 IS INITIAL.

          lv_tottrlimptoiva8 = lwa_zta1_2-tottrlimptoiva8.

        ENDIF.

        IF lwa_zta1_2-tottrlbaseiva8 IS INITIAL
          AND NOT lv_tottrlbaseiva8 IS INITIAL.

          lwa_zta1_2-tottrlbaseiva8 = lv_tottrlbaseiva8.

        ENDIF.

        IF lwa_zta1_2-tottrlimptoiva8 IS INITIAL
          AND NOT lv_tottrlimptoiva8 IS INITIAL.

          lwa_zta1_2-tottrlimptoiva8 = lv_tottrlimptoiva8.

        ENDIF.

*     End of
        CONCATENATE 'CP' v_cons
               INTO lwa_zta1_2-numcomp.
        MODIFY t_zta1_2 FROM lwa_zta1_2 INDEX sy-tabix.
        MOVE lwa_zta1_2-rfc_cl TO lv_rfc.
        MOVE lwa_zta1_2-ztpago TO lv_ztpago.
    "$$
    "$$
    "$$
*          ENDIF. "VYGC
      ENDLOOP.
*       END OF MOD WLECEA.
**   ENDLOOP.

*Asignar tablas para generar xml y pdf
      lt_clientes[] = t_zta1_2[].
      lt_pagos[]    = t_zta1_2[].
      lt_fac[]      = t_zta1_2[].

      SORT lt_clientes
        BY (v_xx8).

      SORT lt_pagos
        BY (v_xx8)
           bukrs
           ztpago
           anio_pago.

      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_pagos
                            COMPARING (v_xx8)
                                      bukrs
                                      ztpago
                                      anio_pago.

      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_clientes
                            COMPARING (v_xx8).

      SORT lt_fac
        BY bukrs
           ztpago
           anio_pago.
* Begin of
      READ TABLE lt_pagos ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<f_pagos>) INDEX 1.

      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.

        IF <f_pagos>-tottrlbaseiva8 IS INITIAL
          AND NOT lv_tottrlbaseiva8 IS INITIAL.

          <f_pagos>-tottrlbaseiva8 = lv_tottrlbaseiva8.

        ENDIF.

        IF <f_pagos>-tottrlimptoiva8 IS INITIAL
          AND NOT lv_tottrlimptoiva8 IS INITIAL.

          <f_pagos>-tottrlimptoiva8 = lv_tottrlimptoiva8.

        ENDIF.

        MODIFY lt_pagos FROM <f_pagos> INDEX sy-tabix.

      ENDIF.

      CLEAR: lv_tottrlbaseiva16, lv_tottrlbaseiva8,
      lv_tottrlimptoiva16, lv_tottrlimptoiva8.
* End of
      SORT t_zta1_2
        BY (v_xx8).

*Declaraciones SD------------------------------------------------------
      PERFORM f_datos_sd.

*Lectura de clientes---------------------------------------------------
      LOOP AT lt_clientes INTO lwa_clientes.

        wa_zta1_cp1-seleccion = lwa_clientes-seleccion.
        wa_zta1_cp1-bukrs     = lwa_clientes-bukrs.
        wa_zta1_cp1-kunnr     = lwa_clientes-kunnr.
        wa_zta1_cp1-rfc_cl    = lwa_clientes-rfc_cl.
        wa_zta1_cp1-nombre    = lwa_clientes-nombre.

        lwa_datos_mail-cliente = lwa_clientes-nombre.

*INI VYGC
            IF p_sust = c_x.

              LOOP AT t_ztssaldos
                 INTO lwa_saldos
                WHERE zcanc = c_x.

                APPEND lwa_saldos
                    TO lt_saldos.

              ENDLOOP.

            ENDIF.
*FIN VYGC

            MODIFY ztssaldos
              FROM TABLE lt_saldos[].

            IF v_x19 = c_x.

**Enviar correo con PDF y XML(el que devuelve el pac) adjuntos
              PERFORM f_genera_correo
                USING lv_size_pdf
                      lv_pdf_xstring
                      lv_xml_string
                      lv_nom_pdf
                      lv_nom_xml
                      t_datos_mail
                      wa_zcfdit002-fechatimbrado
                      lwa_clientes-bukrs
                      lwa_clientes-kunnr. "29 VYGC

            ENDIF.

          ENDIF.

          v_log = c_x.

          REFRESH t_zta1_cp1_2[].
          REFRESH t_datos_mail[].
          REFRESH t_xml[].

        ENDLOOP.
        "Guardo LOG SGL1.
      ENDLOOP."""""""""""""""""""nuevo
      "=== Llevar errores del PAC a ALV (show_pac_all_errors) ===

      IF lv_response IS NOT INITIAL.


      ENDIF.

***SE MUESTRA EL ERROR O EXITO DEL TIMBRADO

      IF lv_response IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM show_xml_popup USING lv_response 'Respuesta del PAC'.
      ENDIF.





****
****    CALL METHOD lo_logger->save.
****    CALL METHOD lo_logger->get_as_bapiret1
****      RECEIVING
****        rt_messages = lt_messages.
****
*****    CALL FUNCTION 'BAL_DSP_LOG_DISPLAY'.

*Mostrar LOG
      PERFORM f_alv_el
        USING lc_gui_log.

ENDFORM.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------
* show_xml_popup  [sintaxis vieja; sin SCMS_*]
* Muestra un XSTRING (XML) en popup SAP GUI, partido en líneas de 255.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------
FORM show_xml_popup USING    iv_xml_xstr TYPE xstring
                             iv_title    TYPE string.

  TYPES: ty_line255 TYPE c LENGTH 255.

  DATA: lv_in_str     TYPE string,
        lv_xml_err    TYPE string,
        lv_xml_pretty TYPE string,
        lv_nl         TYPE string,
        lv_end_tag    TYPE string VALUE '</InnerErrors>',
        lv_end_len    TYPE i,
        lv_off_start  TYPE i,
        lv_off_end    TYPE i,
        lv_len        TYPE i.

  DATA: lt_lines TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_line255,
        lv_line  TYPE ty_line255,
        lv_one   TYPE string,
        lv_pos   TYPE i.

  lv_nl = cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline.

  " 1) XSTRING -> STRING (UTF-8)
  PERFORM zconv_xstr_to_str USING iv_xml_xstr CHANGING lv_in_str.

  " 2) Extraer <InnerErrors>...</InnerErrors> (case-insensitive); si no hay, tomar desde el primer '<'
  CLEAR: lv_xml_err, lv_off_start, lv_off_end.
  lv_off_start = -1.
  FIND REGEX '<[Ii]nner[Ee]rrors' IN lv_in_str MATCH OFFSET lv_off_start.

  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    FIND REGEX '</[Ii]nner[Ee]rrors>' IN lv_in_str MATCH OFFSET lv_off_end.
    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND lv_off_end > lv_off_start.
      lv_end_len = strlen( lv_end_tag ). " longitud literal del cierre
      lv_len = lv_off_end - lv_off_start + lv_end_len.
      lv_xml_err = lv_in_str+lv_off_start(lv_len).
    ELSE.
      lv_xml_err = lv_in_str+lv_off_start.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF '<' IN lv_in_str MATCH OFFSET lv_off_start.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      lv_xml_err = lv_in_str+lv_off_start.
    ELSE.
      lv_xml_err = lv_in_str. " sin XML; muestra tal cual
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  " 3) Pretty print (indentado legible)
  PERFORM f_pretty_xml_from_text USING lv_xml_err CHANGING lv_xml_pretty.
  IF lv_xml_pretty IS INITIAL.
    lv_xml_pretty = lv_xml_err.
  ENDIF.

  " 4) Normalizar saltos
  REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF cl_abap_char_utilities=>cr_lf IN lv_xml_pretty
         WITH lv_nl.

  " 5) Cargar líneas SIN envolver (no romper etiquetas/atributos)
  "    Sólo cortamos si alguna línea > 255 chars.
  DATA: lt_raw TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF string.
  SPLIT lv_xml_pretty AT lv_nl INTO TABLE lt_raw.

  LOOP AT lt_raw INTO lv_one.
    IF lv_one IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR lv_line.
      APPEND lv_line TO lt_lines.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

    lv_pos = 0.
    WHILE lv_pos < strlen( lv_one ).
      CLEAR lv_line.
      " tramo de hasta 255 caracteres
      IF ( strlen( lv_one ) - lv_pos ) > 255.
        lv_line = lv_one+lv_pos(255).
      ELSE.
        lv_line = lv_one+lv_pos.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND lv_line TO lt_lines.
      lv_pos = lv_pos + 255.
    ENDWHILE.
  ENDLOOP.

  " 6) Popup amplio (scroll horizontal dispo por ancho grande)
  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_WITH_TABLE_DISPLAY_OK'
    EXPORTING
      startpos_col = 2
      startpos_row = 2
      endpos_col   = 220
      endpos_row   = 35
      titletext    = iv_title
    TABLES
      valuetab     = lt_lines.

ENDFORM.




*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  FORM f_pretty_xml_from_text  (compatible 750)
*  - Toma un texto (que puede traer basura antes del XML)
*  - Extrae el XML desde el primer '<'
*  - Inserta saltos de línea entre tags y aplica sangrías
*  - Si algo falla, devuelve el XML tal cual
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_pretty_xml_from_text
  USING    iv_text        TYPE string
  CHANGING cv_xml_pretty  TYPE string.

  DATA: lv_off          TYPE i,
        lv_xml_only     TYPE string,
        lv_work         TYPE string,
        lv_nl           TYPE string,
        lv_repl         TYPE string,
        lt_lines        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF string WITH EMPTY KEY,
        lv_line         TYPE string,
        lv_out          TYPE string,
        lv_indent       TYPE i VALUE 0,
        lv_pad          TYPE string,
        lv_len          TYPE i,
        lv_off_last2    TYPE i,
        lv_is_closing   TYPE abap_bool,
        lv_is_selfclose TYPE abap_bool,
        lv_is_special   TYPE abap_bool,   " <?  <!  <!--  <![CDATA[
        lv_is_opening   TYPE abap_bool.

  CLEAR cv_xml_pretty.
  lv_nl = cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline.

  " 1) Extraer solo el XML desde el primer '<'
  FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF '<' IN iv_text MATCH OFFSET lv_off.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    lv_xml_only = iv_text+lv_off.
  ELSE.
    cv_xml_pretty = iv_text.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  " 2) Normalizar: quitar CR/LF/TAB y separar '><' en líneas
  lv_work = lv_xml_only.
  REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF REGEX '[\r\n\t]+' IN lv_work WITH ''.
  CONCATENATE '>' lv_nl '<' INTO lv_repl.
  REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '><' IN lv_work WITH lv_repl.

  " 3) Indentar línea por línea
  SPLIT lv_work AT lv_nl INTO TABLE lt_lines.

  LOOP AT lt_lines INTO lv_line.
    SHIFT lv_line LEFT  DELETING LEADING space.
    SHIFT lv_line RIGHT DELETING TRAILING space.
    IF lv_line IS INITIAL.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

    lv_len = strlen( lv_line ).

    CLEAR: lv_is_closing, lv_is_selfclose, lv_is_special, lv_is_opening.

    IF lv_len >= 2 AND lv_line(2) = '</'.
      lv_is_closing = abap_true.
    ELSEIF lv_len >= 2 AND ( lv_line(2) = '<?' OR lv_line(2) = '<!' ).
      lv_is_special = abap_true.            " declaración, comentario, DOCTYPE, CDATA...
    ELSEIF lv_len >= 2.
      lv_off_last2 = lv_len - 2.
      IF lv_off_last2 >= 0 AND lv_line+lv_off_last2(2) = '/>'.
        lv_is_selfclose = abap_true.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF lv_len >= 1 AND lv_line(1) = '<'
       AND lv_is_closing   = abap_false
       AND lv_is_selfclose = abap_false
       AND lv_is_special   = abap_false.
      lv_is_opening = abap_true.
    ENDIF.

    " Para cierre: bajar indent antes de imprimir
    IF lv_is_closing = abap_true AND lv_indent > 0.
      lv_indent = lv_indent - 1.
    ENDIF.

    " Padding
    CLEAR lv_pad.
    DO lv_indent TIMES.
      CONCATENATE lv_pad '  ' INTO lv_pad.   " 2 espacios por nivel
    ENDDO.

    CONCATENATE lv_out lv_pad lv_line lv_nl INTO lv_out.

    " Para apertura: subir indent después de imprimir
    IF lv_is_opening = abap_true.
      lv_indent = lv_indent + 1.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF lv_out IS INITIAL.
    cv_xml_pretty = lv_xml_only.
  ELSE.
    cv_xml_pretty = lv_out.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.



*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GENERA_CORREO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Armado de correo con archivos XML y PDF
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_genera_correo
  USING pv_size_pdf    TYPE so_obj_len
        pv_pdfxs       TYPE xstring
        pv_xml_string  TYPE string
        pv_nom_pdf     TYPE so_obj_des
        pv_nom_xml     TYPE so_obj_des
        pt_datos_mail  TYPE ty_tt_datos_mail
        pwa_fetim      TYPE zcfdit002-fechatimbrado
        pwa_bukrs      TYPE zcfdit002-bukrs
        pv_cliente     TYPE zta1_cp1-kunnr. "29 VYGC

  DATA lt_email TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zsdmxt020.
  DATA lt_binary TYPE solix_tab WITH HEADER LINE.
  DATA lt_msj TYPE bcsy_text.

  DATA : t_data TYPE TABLE OF x255.
  DATA : ls_line_pdf TYPE x255.

  DATA lt_bin_pdf TYPE solix_tab.

  DATA lwa_xml TYPE ty_xml.

  DATA lv_xml TYPE stringval. "string.
  DATA lv_xmlxs TYPE xstring.
  DATA lv_size_xml TYPE so_obj_len.
  DATA lv_bin_filesize_xml TYPE i.
  DATA lv_asunto TYPE so_obj_des.
  DATA lv_fetim TYPE char10.
  DATA: v_path TYPE zpath.
  DATA lv_length TYPE i.
  DATA lv_pdf LIKE LINE OF lt_bin_pdf.
  CONSTANTS lc_amp(2) TYPE c VALUE '&&'.

**Pasar XML a string
*  CONCATENATE w_output-timbrar_cfdiresult-base-xml
*              lwa_xml-linea
*         INTO lv_xml
*    SEPARATED BY space.

  lv_xml = pv_xml_string .

  CONDENSE lv_xml.

*Convierte cadena string en Xstring
  CALL FUNCTION 'SCMS_STRING_TO_XSTRING'
    EXPORTING
      text   = lv_xml
    IMPORTING
      buffer = lv_xmlxs
    EXCEPTIONS
      failed = 1
      OTHERS = 2.

  IF sy-subrc <> 0.

    MESSAGE TEXT-e14
       TYPE c_e.

  ELSE.

*Convierte XSTRING a Binario
    CALL FUNCTION 'SCMS_XSTRING_TO_BINARY'
      EXPORTING
        buffer          = lv_xmlxs
        append_to_table = ' '
      IMPORTING
        output_length   = lv_bin_filesize_xml
      TABLES
        binary_tab      = lt_binary.

    lv_size_xml = lv_bin_filesize_xml.

  ENDIF.

*Listado de correos
  PERFORM f_mail_data
    CHANGING lt_email
             pv_cliente. "29 VYGC

*Cuerpo del correo
  PERFORM f_body_mail
    USING    pt_datos_mail
    CHANGING lt_msj.

*Asunto del correo
  lv_asunto =  TEXT-z02.

  lv_fetim = pwa_fetim+0(10).

  REPLACE lc_amp
     INTO lv_asunto
     WITH lv_fetim.

  DATA w_zftp TYPE zftp.

  DATA: v_filename2 TYPE ufpathdat,
        lv_hdl      TYPE i,
        lv_subrc    TYPE i.
  v_filename2 =  pv_nom_pdf.

**           Conectar FTP
*  PERFORM f_conection_ftp
* CHANGING lv_hdl
*          w_zftp
**          lv_subrc.
*          lv_subrc
*          pwa_bukrs.
*
*
*  CONCATENATE w_zftp-zpath
*              v_filename2"pi_filename
*         INTO v_path.
*
*  PERFORM f_upload_file_ftp_x
*   TABLES t_data
*    USING lv_hdl
*          v_path
*          lv_subrc
*    CHANGING lv_length.
*
*  pv_size_pdf = lv_length.
*
**       Rutina para desconectar el FTP
*  PERFORM f_disconnet_ftp
*    CHANGING lv_hdl.
*
*  LOOP AT t_data INTO ls_line_pdf.
*
*    lv_pdf-line = ls_line_pdf.
*
*    APPEND lv_pdf TO lt_bin_pdf.
*  ENDLOOP.


  IF lt_email[] IS NOT INITIAL AND
     lv_xmlxs IS NOT INITIAL." AND
*     pv_pdfxs IS NOT INITIAL.

*Función que envia correo
    CALL FUNCTION 'ZSD_ENVIO_MAIL'
      EXPORTING
        obj_descr   = lv_asunto
        envio_xml   = c_x
        envio_pdf   = c_x
        size_pdf    = pv_size_pdf
        pdf_xstring = pv_pdfxs
        size_xml    = lv_size_xml
        xml_xstring = lv_xmlxs
        nom_xml     = pv_nom_xml
        nom_pdf     = pv_nom_pdf
        conv_pdf    = '' "c_x
      TABLES
        emails      = lt_email
        t_msj       = lt_msj
        pdf_bin     = lt_bin_pdf.

    IF sy-subrc = 0.

      MESSAGE TEXT-e17
         TYPE c_s.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_CAMPOS_REQ
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Campos requieridos por reporte
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  <--  PV_BAND        Indicador de error
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_campos_req
  CHANGING pv_band TYPE c.

*Campos requeridos
  IF s_bukrs IS INITIAL.

*La sociedad es requerida
    MESSAGE TEXT-e06
       TYPE c_s
    DISPLAY LIKE c_e.

    pv_band = c_x.

  ENDIF.

  IF s_monat IS INITIAL.

    IF p_emit = c_x .

      IF p_pmas = c_x.

*El periodo es requerido
        MESSAGE TEXT-e07
           TYPE c_s
        DISPLAY LIKE c_e.

        pv_band = c_x.

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

  IF s_gjahr IS INITIAL.

    IF p_emit = c_x OR
       p_renv = c_x.

*El ejercicio es requerido
      MESSAGE TEXT-e08
         TYPE c_s
      DISPLAY LIKE c_e.

      pv_band = c_x.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

*Campos requeridos Emision
  IF p_emit = c_x.

    IF p_pind = c_x.

      IF s_pagind IS INITIAL.

*El documento de pago es requerido
        MESSAGE TEXT-e10
           TYPE c_s
        DISPLAY LIKE c_e.

        pv_band = c_x.

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

*Campos requeridos Sustitución
  IF p_sust = c_x.

    PERFORM f_req_sust
      CHANGING pv_band.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_VERIF
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Verificación
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_verif .

  DATA lv_band TYPE c.

  REFRESH: t_zta1_aux[],
           t_zta1_cp1[],
           t_zta1_2[],
           t_zta1_cp1_2[],
           t_xml[].

  REFRESH t_ztssaldos[]. "VYGC
  REFRESH t_ztssaldos2[]. "VYGC

  CLEAR v_log.

  PERFORM f_campos_req
    CHANGING lv_band.

  IF lv_band NE c_x.

*Clasificación de clases de documento
    SELECT *
      FROM zttipodoc
      INTO TABLE t_tdoc
     WHERE bukrs IN s_bukrs.

      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND
         t_tdoc[] IS NOT INITIAL.

        SORT t_tdoc
          BY bukrs
             blart.

      ENDIF.

*Parametrizaciones:
      PERFORM f_param.

*Emision
      IF p_emit = c_x.

        PERFORM f_veri_emis.

*Sustitución
      ELSEIF p_sust = c_x.

*   Validar bloque de sustitución
        PERFORM f_valida_sust
          CHANGING lv_band.

        IF lv_band NE c_x.

*   Verificar información
          PERFORM f_veri_sust.

        ENDIF.

*Re-envio
      ELSEIF p_renv = c_x.

        PERFORM f_veri_renv.

*Reporte
      ELSEIF p_repo = c_x.

        PERFORM f_veri_repo.

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_MAIL_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Obtiene Correos
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_mail_data
  CHANGING pt_email TYPE ty_tt_zsdmxt020
           pv_cliente TYPE zta1_cp1-kunnr. "29 VYGC

*****  TYPES: BEGIN OF ty_correos,
*****           bukrs     TYPE ztcorreoscp-bukrs,
*****           kunnr     TYPE ztcorreoscp-kunnr,
*****           secuencia TYPE ztcorreoscp-secuencia,
*****           name      TYPE ztcorreoscp-name,
*****           correo    TYPE ztcorreoscp-correo,
*****         END OF ty_correos.
*****
*****  DATA lt_correos TYPE TABLE OF ty_correos.
*****
*****  DATA w_correos TYPE ty_correos.
*****  DATA lwa_email TYPE zsdmxt020.
*****
******Tabla de correos
*****  SELECT bukrs
*****         kunnr
*****         secuencia
*****         name
*****         correo
*****    FROM ztcorreoscp
*****    INTO TABLE lt_correos
****** FOR ALL ENTRIES IN t_zta1_2
******   WHERE bukrs = t_zta1_2-bukrs
******     AND kunnr = t_zta1_2-kunnr.
*****   WHERE bukrs IN s_bukrs "29 VYGC
*****     AND kunnr = pv_cliente. "29 VYGC
*****
*****  IF sy-subrc = 0.
*****
*****    SORT lt_correos
*****      BY bukrs
*****         kunnr.
*****
*****    LOOP AT lt_correos
*****       INTO w_correos.
*****
*****      lwa_email-zmail = w_correos-correo.
*****
*****      APPEND lwa_email
*****          TO pt_email.
*****
*****    ENDLOOP.
*****
*****  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_VALID_PAGO_COMP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Validar que el pago se envie completo
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_valid_pago_comp
  CHANGING pv_band TYPE c.

  DATA t_zta1_2_aux TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zsa1_cp1.

  DATA lwa_zta1_2 TYPE zsa1_cp1 .
  DATA lwa_zta1_2_aux TYPE zsa1_cp1.
  DATA lwa_zta1_aux TYPE zsa1_cp1.

  DATA lv_band2 TYPE c.

  t_zta1_2_aux[] = t_zta1_2[].

  SORT t_zta1_2_aux
    BY ztpago
       anio_pago.

  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM t_zta1_2_aux
                        COMPARING ztpago
                                  anio_pago.

  SORT t_zta1_2
    BY bukrs
       kunnr
       ztpago
       anio_pago
       ztfactrel
       anio_fac.

*Leer pagos
  LOOP AT t_zta1_2_aux
     INTO lwa_zta1_2_aux.

*Leer registros correspondientes al pago
    LOOP AT t_zta1_aux
       INTO lwa_zta1_aux
      WHERE ztpago = lwa_zta1_2_aux-ztpago
        AND estatus = c_amarillo
        AND seleccion = c_x.

      lv_band2 = c_x.

      EXIT.

    ENDLOOP.

*Leer registros correspondientes al pago
    LOOP AT t_zta1_aux
       INTO lwa_zta1_aux
      WHERE ztpago = lwa_zta1_2_aux-ztpago
        AND ( estatus = c_verde
         OR estatus = c_amarillo ).

*Validar que el pago este completo en lo que se envio a emitir
      READ TABLE t_zta1_2
            INTO lwa_zta1_2
        WITH KEY bukrs     = lwa_zta1_aux-bukrs
                 kunnr     = lwa_zta1_aux-kunnr
                 ztpago    = lwa_zta1_aux-ztpago
                 anio_pago = lwa_zta1_aux-anio_pago
                 ztfactrel = lwa_zta1_aux-ztfactrel
                 anio_fac  = lwa_zta1_aux-anio_fac
          BINARY SEARCH.

      IF sy-subrc NE 0.

        pv_band = c_x.

        MESSAGE TEXT-e13
           TYPE c_s
        DISPLAY LIKE c_e.

      ENDIF.

    ENDLOOP.

    IF lv_band2 = c_x.

*Leer registros correspondientes al pago
      LOOP AT t_zta1_aux
         INTO lwa_zta1_aux
        WHERE ztpago = lwa_zta1_2_aux-ztpago
          AND estatus = c_amarillo.

*Validar que el pago este completo en lo que se envio a emitir
        READ TABLE t_zta1_2
              INTO lwa_zta1_2
          WITH KEY bukrs     = lwa_zta1_aux-bukrs
                   kunnr     = lwa_zta1_aux-kunnr
                   ztpago    = lwa_zta1_aux-ztpago
                   anio_pago = lwa_zta1_aux-anio_pago
                   ztfactrel = lwa_zta1_aux-ztfactrel
                   anio_fac  = lwa_zta1_aux-anio_fac
            BINARY SEARCH.

        IF sy-subrc NE 0.

          pv_band = c_x.

          MESSAGE TEXT-e13
             TYPE c_s
          DISPLAY LIKE c_e.

        ENDIF.

      ENDLOOP.

    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP. "pagos

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_REQ_SUST
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Campos requeridos para sustitución
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_PV_BAND  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_req_sust
  CHANGING pv_band TYPE c.

  CONSTANTS lc_1 TYPE c VALUE '1'. "VYGC

  IF s_docanu[] IS INITIAL.

*Falta documento de pago erroneo, no es posible sustitución
    MESSAGE TEXT-e21
       TYPE c_s
    DISPLAY LIKE c_e.

    pv_band = c_x.

*  ELSEIF s_docpag IS INITIAL.
*
**Falta documento correcto
*    MESSAGE text-e18
*       TYPE c_s
*    DISPLAY LIKE c_e.
*
*    pv_band = c_x.

  ELSEIF p_uuidpa IS INITIAL.

*Falta UUID de pago erroneo
    MESSAGE TEXT-e22
       TYPE c_s
    DISPLAY LIKE c_e.

    pv_band = c_x.

**El año de anulación es requerido
*  ELSEIF s_gjahra IS INITIAL.
*
*    MESSAGE text-e19
*       TYPE c_s
*    DISPLAY LIKE c_e.
*
*    pv_band = c_x.

*El año de pago es requerido
  ELSEIF s_gjahrs IS INITIAL.

    IF s_docpag IS NOT INITIAL.

      MESSAGE TEXT-e20
         TYPE c_s
      DISPLAY LIKE c_e.

      pv_band = c_x.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_BODY_MAIL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_body_mail
  USING    pt_datos_mail TYPE ty_tt_datos_mail
  CHANGING pt_msj        TYPE bcsy_text.

  DATA lwa_msj TYPE soli.
  DATA lwa_datos_mail TYPE ty_datos_mail.

  LOOP AT pt_datos_mail
     INTO lwa_datos_mail.

    IF sy-tabix = 1.

*Cliente
      lwa_msj-line = lwa_datos_mail-cliente.

      APPEND lwa_msj
          TO pt_msj.

*Adjunto encontrará los archivos XML y PDF de los pagos: (ZTPago)
      lwa_msj-line = TEXT-z06.

      APPEND lwa_msj
          TO pt_msj.

    ENDIF.

*Pago
    lwa_msj-line = lwa_datos_mail-pago.

    APPEND lwa_msj
        TO pt_msj.

  ENDLOOP.

*Saludos.
  lwa_msj-line = TEXT-z04.

  APPEND lwa_msj
      TO pt_msj.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F_BOTONES
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_botones .

  DATA lv_band TYPE c.

  CASE sy-ucomm.

*Ir a transacción FSPO
    WHEN c_veri.

      PERFORM f_verif.

*Bancos
    WHEN c_banc.

      PERFORM f_tbl_bancos.

*Tipo de póliza
    WHEN c_tpol.

      PERFORM f_tpoliza.

*Monedas
    WHEN c_mone.

      PERFORM f_tmoneda.

*Datos clientes
    WHEN c_datc.

      PERFORM f_tdatos_cliente.

    WHEN ''.

      IF p_sust = c_x.

*       Validar bloque de sustitución
        PERFORM f_valida_sust
          CHANGING lv_band.

      ENDIF.

  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.

*&      Form  F_BUSCAR_FACTURA_PARCIAL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  pwa_bsad_cpt_aux        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_buscar_factura_parcial USING pwa_bsad_cpt_aux TYPE ty_bsad.

  DATA: lt_bsid1 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bsid.

  DATA: lwa_bsid1   TYPE bsid,
        lwa_bsid2   TYPE bsid,
        lwa_bsad    TYPE ty_bsad,
        lwa_bseg    TYPE ty_bseg_uuid,
        lwa_vbrk    TYPE ty_vbrk,
        lwa_bkpf_sd TYPE ty_bkpf_sd.

* BEGIN OF MOD WLECEA 21.12.2017
  DATA: lv_ukurs    TYPE ukurs_curr,
        lwa_bkpf_tc TYPE ty_bkpf_tc,
        lv_date     TYPE datum,
        lv_vbeln    TYPE vbrk-vbeln,
        lv_zterm    TYPE vbrk-zterm,
        lv_lines    TYPE sy-tabix.
* END OF MOD WLECEA 21.12.2017

*INI VYGC 01.15.2017
*  SELECT SINGLE *
  SELECT *
*    INTO  lwa_bsid1
    INTO TABLE lt_bsid1
*FIN VYGC 01.15.2017
    FROM bsid
    WHERE bukrs EQ pwa_bsad_cpt_aux-bukrs AND
*          kunnr eq pwa_bsad_cpt_aux-kunnr AND
          gjahr EQ pwa_bsad_cpt_aux-gjahr AND
          belnr EQ pwa_bsad_cpt_aux-belnr.

    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.

*INI VYGC 01.15.2017
      LOOP AT lt_bsid1
         INTO lwa_bsid1.
*FIN VYGC 01.15.2017

        SELECT SINGLE *
          INTO lwa_bsid2
          FROM bsid
          WHERE bukrs EQ lwa_bsid1-bukrs AND
                gjahr EQ lwa_bsid1-rebzj AND
                belnr EQ lwa_bsid1-rebzg.

          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            CLEAR lwa_bsad.
            lwa_bsad-bukrs = lwa_bsid1-bukrs.
            lwa_bsad-kunnr = lwa_bsid1-kunnr.

            lwa_bsad-umskz = lwa_bsid1-umskz.

            lwa_bsad-augbl = pwa_bsad_cpt_aux-augbl.
            lwa_bsad-zuonr = lwa_bsid1-zuonr.
            lwa_bsad-gjahr = lwa_bsid2-gjahr. "lwa_bsid1-gjahr VYGC
            lwa_bsad-belnr = lwa_bsid2-belnr.
            lwa_bsad-buzei = lwa_bsid2-buzei.
            lwa_bsad-budat = lwa_bsid2-budat.
            lwa_bsad-cpudt = lwa_bsid2-cpudt.
            lwa_bsad-waers = lwa_bsid2-waers.

*INI VYGC 01.15.2017

*        lwa_bsad-wrbtr = pwa_bsad_cpt_aux-wrbtr. "lwa_bsid1-wrbtr.
            lwa_bsad-dmbtr = lwa_bsid1-dmbtr.
            lwa_bsad-wrbtr = lwa_bsid1-wrbtr.
*FIN VYGC 01.15.2017
            lwa_bsad-auggj = pwa_bsad_cpt_aux-auggj.
            lwa_bsad-flag  = 'X'.
            lwa_bsad-imp_dr = lwa_bsid2-wrbtr.
            lwa_bsad-impdrml = lwa_bsid2-dmbtr.
            APPEND lwa_bsad TO t_bsad_cpt_aux.
* BEGIN OF MOD WLECEA 21.12.2017

*        CONCATENATE lwa_bsad-augdt+6(2)
*                    lwa_bsad-augdt+4(2)
*                    lwa_bsad-augdt+0(4)
*               INTO lv_date.
*    Tomar fecha de documento DR

*INI VYGC 05.08.2020

*FIN VYGC 05.08.2020
            SELECT SINGLE kursf
              INTO lv_ukurs

              WHERE bukrs EQ lwa_bsad-bukrs AND
                    belnr EQ lwa_bsad-belnr AND
                    gjahr EQ lwa_bsad-gjahr.

              MOVE: lwa_bsad-bukrs TO lwa_bkpf_tc-bukrs,
                    lwa_bsad-belnr TO lwa_bkpf_tc-belnr,

*INI VYGC 05.08.2020
*      ELSE.

*                    lwa_bsad-budat+0(4)
*               INTO lv_date.
*
*        CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_INVDT_INPUT'

*            input  = lv_date

*            output = lv_date.

*FIN VYGC 05.08.2020

              MOVE: lwa_bsad-bukrs TO lwa_bkpf_tc-bukrs,
                    lwa_bsad-zuonr TO lwa_bkpf_tc-belnr,
                    lwa_bsad-gjahr TO lwa_bkpf_tc-gjahr,

              APPEND lwa_bkpf_tc TO t_bkpf_tc.
              SORT t_bkpf_tc BY bukrs belnr gjahr.

* END OF MOD WLECEA 21.12.2017
              CLEAR lwa_bseg.
              lwa_bseg-bukrs = lwa_bsid1-bukrs.
              lwa_bseg-belnr = lwa_bsid2-belnr.
              lwa_bseg-gjahr = lwa_bsid2-gjahr.
              lwa_bseg-vbeln = lwa_bsid1-zuonr.
              APPEND lwa_bseg TO t_bseg_uuid.
              SORT t_bseg_uuid BY bukrs belnr gjahr.

*    Arrastre de saldos
              SELECT *
                FROM ztssaldos
                APPENDING TABLE t_ztssaldos
                    WHERE ztssociedad = lwa_bsad-bukrs
                      AND ztsfactura  = lwa_bsad-belnr
                      AND ztsaniodr   = lwa_bsad-gjahr.
                SORT t_ztssaldos
                  BY ztssociedad
                     ztsfactura
                     ztsaniodr
                     ztscontador DESCENDING.

                SELECT bukrs
                       belnr
                       gjahr
                       awtyp
                       awkey
                  FROM bkpf
             APPENDING TABLE t_bkpf_sd

                 WHERE bukrs EQ lwa_bsid2-bukrs
                   AND belnr EQ lwa_bsid2-belnr
                   AND gjahr EQ lwa_bsid2-gjahr.

                  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.

                          INTO lwa_bkpf_sd
                          INDEX lv_lines.

                    IF sy-subrc = 0.
                      lv_vbeln = lwa_bkpf_sd-awkey.
                    ENDIF.

*Factura: Datos de cabecera
                    SELECT vbeln
                           kunag

                     WHERE vbeln = lv_vbeln.

                         WHERE zterm = t_vbrk-zterm.

                          IF sy-subrc = 0.

                          ENDIF.

                        ENDIF.

                      ENDIF.

                    ENDIF.

*INI VYGC 01.15.2017
                  ENDLOOP.
*FIN VYGC 01.15.2017
                  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM t_bsad_cpt_aux COMPARING ALL FIELDS.

                ENDIF.

*        augbl
*        auggj
*        zuonr.

                SORT t_bkpf_sd
                  BY bukrs
                     belnr
                     gjahr.

                SORT t_vbrk
                  BY vbeln
                     bukrs.

                SORT t_payterms
                  BY zterm.


ENDFORM.                    " F_BUSCAR_FACTURA_PARCIAL

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_VALIDA_DATOS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_leer_tim_sql
 CHANGING pv_ruta       TYPE string
          pv_text_stat  TYPE string
          pv_stat       TYPE zta1_cp1-estatus
          pwa_zcfdit002 TYPE zcfdit002
          pv_nom_xml    TYPE so_obj_des
          pv_sello      TYPE string
          pv_cert       TYPE string
          pv_nocert     TYPE string.

  TYPES:
    BEGIN OF ty_t300_cfdi,
*      301facturaid        TYPE char30,
      301facturaid        TYPE i,
      301version          TYPE char10,
      301uuid             TYPE char36,
      301fechatimbrado    TYPE char8,
      301horatimbrado     TYPE char6,
      301sellocfd(500)    TYPE c,
      301nocertificadosat TYPE char50,
      301sellosat(500)    TYPE c,
      301cosat(500)       TYPE c,
      301rfcprovcert      TYPE char12,
    END OF ty_t300_cfdi.

  CONSTANTS: lc_extragenerales49(16) TYPE c VALUE 'extraGenerales49',
             lc_extragenerales50(16) TYPE c VALUE 'extraGenerales50',
             lc_folio(05)            TYPE c VALUE 'folio',
             lc_bs                   TYPE c VALUE '\',
             lc_ext(04)              TYPE c VALUE '.xml',
             lc_txt(04)              TYPE c VALUE '.TXT'.

  DATA: lwa_txt             TYPE ty_txt,
        lv_ruta             TYPE string,
*        lv_flag          TYPE c,
        lv_str              TYPE string,
        lv_xml              TYPE string,
*        lv_db            TYPE dbcon-con_name VALUE 'DETECNO2',
        lv_db               TYPE dbcon-con_name VALUE 'DETECNO',
*        lv_facturaid     TYPE char30,
        lv_facturaid        TYPE i,
        lv_folio(40)        TYPE c,
*        lv_statusid      TYPE char8,
        lv_statusid         TYPE i,
*        lv_rechazoid     TYPE char8,
        lv_rechazoid        TYPE i,
        lv_nocertificado    TYPE char20,
        lv_certificado(500) TYPE c,
*        lv_rej_code      TYPE cfdi_rejcode_mxt-rejcode_txt,
        lwa_t300_cfdi       TYPE ty_t300_cfdi.

  DATA: lv_exc_ref    TYPE REF TO cx_sy_native_sql_error,
        lv_error_text TYPE        string.

*  CLEAR lwa_txt.
*  READ TABLE t_txt
*       INTO lwa_txt
*   WITH KEY id =  lc_extragenerales50.
*
*  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*
*    CONCATENATE '\\PALMEXQASCFDI\CFDiManager-Emision\3-SpoolValidos'
*                lc_bs
*                lwa_txt-valor
*                lc_txt
*           INTO lv_ruta.
*
*    pv_nom_xml  = lwa_txt-valor.
*
*  ENDIF.

*  DO 10 TIMES.
*
*    OPEN DATASET lv_ruta FOR INPUT IN TEXT MODE ENCODING DEFAULT.
*
*    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*      lv_flag = c_x.
*    ENDIF.
*
*    CLOSE DATASET lv_ruta.
*
*    IF lv_flag EQ c_x.
*      EXIT.
*    ELSE.
*      WAIT UP TO 2 SECONDS.
*    ENDIF.
*
*  ENDDO.

*  IF lv_flag EQ c_x.

  CLEAR lwa_txt.
  CLEAR lv_folio.

*  READ TABLE t_txt
*        INTO lwa_txt
*    WITH KEY id =  lc_folio.

  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    CLEAR lwa_txt.
  ELSE.
    lv_folio = lwa_txt-valor.
  ENDIF.

  TRY.

      EXEC SQL.
        CONNECT TO :lv_db AS 'R'
      ENDEXEC.

      EXEC SQL.
        SET CONNECTION 'R'
      ENDEXEC.

      DO 60 TIMES.

        EXEC SQL.
          SELECT MAX([001facturaid])
            INTO :lv_facturaid
            FROM T001_Principal
           WHERE [001folio] = :lv_folio
        ENDEXEC.

        IF lv_facturaid IS INITIAL.
          WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
        ELSE.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.

      ENDDO.

      IF lv_facturaid IS NOT INITIAL.

        DO 60 TIMES.

          EXEC SQL.
            SELECT [001estatusid],
                   [001rechazoid],
                   [001nocertificado],
                   [001certificado]
              INTO :lv_statusid,
                   :lv_rechazoid,
                   :lv_nocertificado,
                   :lv_certificado
              FROM T001_Principal
             WHERE [001facturaid] = :lv_facturaid
          ENDEXEC.

          IF ( lv_statusid EQ 4 )
          OR ( lv_statusid EQ 5 ).
            EXIT.
          ELSE.

            CLEAR: lv_statusid,
                   lv_rechazoid,
                   lv_nocertificado,
                   lv_certificado.

            WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.

          ENDIF.


        ENDDO.

      ELSE.
        pv_text_stat = TEXT-m11.
        pv_stat = c_rojo.

      ENDIF.

      IF lv_statusid EQ 4.

        DO 60 TIMES.

          EXEC SQL.
            SELECT [301facturaid],
                   [301version],
                   [301uuid],
                   [301fechatimbrado],
                   [301horatimbrado],
                   [301sellocfd],
                   [301nocertificadosat],
                   [301sellosat],
                   [301cosat],
                   [301rfcprovcert]
              INTO :lwa_t300_cfdi
              FROM T301_Cfdi
             WHERE [301facturaid]  = :lv_facturaid
          ENDEXEC.

          IF lwa_t300_cfdi IS INITIAL.
            WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
          ELSE.
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.

        ENDDO.

      ELSEIF lv_statusid EQ 5.

*          SELECT SINGLE
*                 rejcode_txt
*            FROM cfdi_rejcode_mxt
*            INTO lv_rej_code
*           WHERE spras EQ sy-langu
*             AND rej_code EQ lv_rechazoid.
*
*          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*            pv_text_stat = lv_rej_code.
*          ENDIF.
        pv_text_stat = TEXT-m11.
        pv_stat = c_rojo.

      ELSE.

        pv_text_stat = TEXT-m10.
        pv_stat = c_rojo.

      ENDIF.

      EXEC SQL.
        SET CONNECTION DEFAULT
      ENDEXEC.

    CATCH cx_sy_native_sql_error INTO lv_exc_ref.

      CLEAR: lv_error_text.
      lv_error_text = lv_exc_ref->get_text( ).
      pv_text_stat = lv_error_text.
      pv_stat = c_rojo.

  ENDTRY.

*  ELSE.
*
*    pv_text_stat = text-m11.
*
*  ENDIF.

  IF lwa_t300_cfdi IS NOT INITIAL.

    CLEAR: pwa_zcfdit002,
           pv_ruta,
           pv_stat,
           pv_nocert,
           pv_cert,
           pv_text_stat.

    pv_ruta = lv_ruta.
    pv_stat = c_verde.
    pv_text_stat = TEXT-m07.

    pwa_zcfdit002-version =  lwa_t300_cfdi-301version.
    pwa_zcfdit002-uuid = lwa_t300_cfdi-301uuid.
    pwa_zcfdit002-sellocfd = lwa_t300_cfdi-301sellocfd.
    pwa_zcfdit002-sellosat = lwa_t300_cfdi-301sellosat.
    pwa_zcfdit002-nocertificadosat = lwa_t300_cfdi-301nocertificadosat.
    pwa_zcfdit002-fechatimbrado = lwa_t300_cfdi-301fechatimbrado.
    pv_nocert = lv_nocertificado.
    pv_cert = lv_certificado.

  ELSE.

    CLEAR: pwa_zcfdit002,
       pv_ruta,
       pv_stat,
       pv_nocert,
       pv_cert.

    pv_stat = c_rojo.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_VALIDA_DATOS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_OBT_XML_TIMBR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_LV_XML  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_obt_xml_timbr_al11
   USING    pv_ruta2 TYPE string
   CHANGING pwa_zcfdit002 TYPE zcfdit002
            pv_text_stat TYPE string
            pv_stat TYPE zta1_cp1-estatus.

  TYPES:
    BEGIN OF ty_xml,
      raw TYPE string,
    END OF ty_xml.

  DATA lt_xml_info TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF smum_xmltb INITIAL SIZE 0.
  DATA lt_xml_tab   TYPE TABLE OF ty_xml INITIAL SIZE 0.

  DATA lwa_zta1_cp1 TYPE zta1_cp1.
  DATA lwa_xml_info LIKE LINE OF lt_xml_info.
  DATA lwa_xml_tab  TYPE ty_xml.
*  DATA lwa_zcfdit002 TYPE zcfdit002.

  DATA lv_xml       TYPE string.

  DATA lv_sello  TYPE string.
  DATA lv_cert   TYPE string.
  DATA lv_nocert TYPE string.

  IF v_x18 = c_al11.

    WAIT UP TO '0.5' SECONDS.

    OPEN DATASET pv_ruta2 FOR INPUT IN TEXT MODE ENCODING DEFAULT
      IGNORING CONVERSION ERRORS.

    IF sy-subrc NE 0.

*    MESSAGE 'Error' TYPE 'E'.

    ELSE.

      DO.
        " leemos el contenido
        READ DATASET pv_ruta2
                INTO lwa_xml_tab-raw.

        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.

          CONDENSE lwa_xml_tab-raw.

          APPEND lwa_xml_tab
              TO lt_xml_tab.

        ELSE.

          EXIT.

        ENDIF.

      ENDDO.

    ENDIF.
    " cerramos el archivo xml del servidor
    CLOSE DATASET pv_ruta2.

    IF NOT lt_xml_tab IS INITIAL.

      LOOP AT lt_xml_tab
         INTO lwa_xml_tab.

        CONCATENATE lv_xml
                    lwa_xml_tab-raw
               INTO lv_xml.

      ENDLOOP.

    ENDIF.

    PERFORM f_timbrado_cfdi
      USING    lv_xml
      CHANGING pwa_zcfdit002
               lv_sello
               lv_cert
               lv_nocert.

  ENDIF.

  IF pwa_zcfdit002-uuid IS NOT INITIAL.

    pv_text_stat = TEXT-m07.
    pv_stat      = c_verde.

  ELSE.

    pv_text_stat = TEXT-m10.
    pv_stat      = c_rojo.

  ENDIF.

*  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    "f_obt_xml_timbr
