**&      Form  F_WS_edicom
**&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
**       text
**----------------------------------------------------------------------*
**  -->  p1        text
**  <--  p2        text
**----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*FORM f_ws_edicom
*  USING    pwa_clientes  TYPE zsa1_cp1
*  CHANGING pv_ruta       TYPE string
*           pv_text_stat  TYPE string
*           pv_stat       TYPE zta1_cp1-estatus
*           pwa_zcfdit002 TYPE zcfdit002
*           pv_nom_xml    TYPE so_obj_des
*           pv_sello      TYPE string
*           pv_cert       TYPE string
*           pv_nocert     TYPE string.
*
*  DATA lt_binary         TYPE solix_tab WITH HEADER LINE.
*  DATA w_oref            TYPE REF TO cx_ai_system_fault.
*  DATA w_oref_edi        TYPE REF TO zcx_ediwin_integration_excepti.
*  DATA w_input           TYPE zpublish_document_request.
*  DATA lv_text           TYPE string.
*  DATA lv_xml_pac        TYPE string.
*  DATA lv_xml_xstring    TYPE xstring.
*  DATA pv_xstring        TYPE xstring.
*  DATA lv_error_idoc(50) TYPE c ##NEEDED.
*  DATA lv_error_edi      TYPE string.
*  DATA lv_dev            TYPE prx_logical_port_name.
*  DATA lv_prd            TYPE prx_logical_port_name.
*
*  PERFORM f_convert_xml
*   TABLES lt_binary
* CHANGING lv_xml_pac
*          lv_xml_xstring.
*
**  Se valida el ambiente actual para la llamada al puerto lógico
*  IF ( sy-sysid EQ v_xx9 )
*  OR ( sy-sysid EQ v_x10 ).
*
*    lv_dev = v_xx4.
*
*    TRY.
*
*        CREATE OBJECT cl_wsdl_test_e
*          EXPORTING
*            logical_port_name = lv_dev.
*
*      CATCH cx_ai_system_fault
*       INTO w_oref.
*
*        lv_text = w_oref->get_text( ).
*        lv_error_idoc = lv_text.
*
*    ENDTRY.
*
*  ELSEIF sy-sysid EQ v_x11.
*
*    lv_prd = v_xx6.
*
*    TRY.
*        CREATE OBJECT cl_wsdl_test_e
*          EXPORTING
*            logical_port_name = lv_prd. "'ZFAC_CFDI_PRD'.
*
*      CATCH cx_ai_system_fault
*       INTO w_oref.
*
*        lv_text = w_oref->get_text( ).
*        lv_error_idoc = lv_text.
*
*    ENDTRY.
*
*  ENDIF.
*
**Llamado al wsdl para timbrar
**llamar ws dev
*  TRY.
*
*      PERFORM f_llena_input_edicom
*        USING lv_xml_xstring "lv_xml_pac  "
*     CHANGING w_input.
*
**      PERFORM f_llena_input_otro
**        USING lv_xml_pac
**     CHANGING w_input.
*
*      CALL METHOD cl_wsdl_test_e->publish_document
*        EXPORTING
*          publish_document_request  = w_input
*        IMPORTING
*          publish_document_response = w_output_e.
*
*    CATCH cx_ai_system_fault
*     INTO w_oref.
*      CLEAR lv_text.
*      lv_text = w_oref->get_text( ).
*      lv_error_idoc = lv_text.
*
*    CATCH zcx_ediwin_integration_excepti
*     INTO w_oref_edi.
*      CLEAR lv_text.
*      lv_text = w_oref_edi->get_text( ).
*      lv_error_idoc = lv_text.
*      CLEAR lv_text.
*      lv_text = w_oref_edi->fault-text.
*      lv_error_edi = lv_text.
*
*
*  ENDTRY.
*
*  IF v_x18 = c_met.
**Guarda informacion de timbrado digital
**  IF  w_output-timbrar_cfdiresult-base-xml IS NOT INITIAL.
*    IF  w_output_e-publish_document_return IS NOT INITIAL.
*
*      PERFORM f_timbrado_cfdi_e
*        USING    lv_xml_xstring   "w_output-publish_document_return
*                 w_output_e-publish_document_return
*        CHANGING pwa_zcfdit002
*                 pv_sello
*                 pv_cert
*                 pv_nocert
*                 pv_xstring.
*
*      pv_text_stat = text-m07.
*      pv_stat      = c_verde.
*
**  ELSEIF w_output-timbrar_cfdiresult-base-base-descripcion IS NOT INITIAL.
*    ELSEIF w_output_e-publish_document_return IS INITIAL.
*
**    pv_text_stat = w_output-timbrar_cfdiresult-base-base-descripcion.
*      pv_text_stat = lv_error_edi.
*      pv_stat      = c_rojo.
*
*    ENDIF.
*
*  ENDIF.
*
**Guardar XML en el servidor
*  PERFORM f_guarda_xml_e
*    USING pwa_clientes
*          pv_xstring    "w_output-publish_document_return
* CHANGING pv_ruta
*          pv_nom_xml.
*
*ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F_CONV_IMP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_WA_ZTA1_AUX_IMP_DR  text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_conv_imp
  USING    p_waers TYPE waers
  CHANGING p_monto TYPE any.

  CONSTANTS lc_jpy(3) TYPE c VALUE 'JPY'.

  DATA lv_monto TYPE bp_wjl.
  DATA lv_monto2 TYPE bapicurr-bapicurr.
  DATA lv_waers TYPE tcurc-waers.

  IF p_waers = lc_jpy.

    lv_monto = p_monto.
    lv_waers = p_waers.

    CALL FUNCTION 'AIA_TOOL_CURRENCY_SAP_TO_EXTER'
      EXPORTING
        i_amount_sap = lv_monto
        i_sap_code   = lv_waers
*       I_OBJECT_ID  =
      IMPORTING
        e_amount_ext = lv_monto2
*       E_SAP_CODE_EXT       =
*       E_ISO_CODE_EXT       =
*       E_RETURN     =
* CHANGING
*       FLG_ERROR    =
      .

    p_monto = lv_monto2.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_CONECTION_FTP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LT_TABLE_BIN  text
*      -->P_LV_FILENAME  text
*      <--P_LV_SUBRC  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_conection_ftp CHANGING p_hdl   TYPE i
                              ps_zftp TYPE zftp
                              p_subrc TYPE sy-subrc
                              p_bukrs TYPE bukrs.
*--
  DATA : lv_key   TYPE i VALUE 26101957,
         lv_mode  TYPE c,
         lv_linea TYPE i,
         lv_len   TYPE i. ",
*
  DATA : lv_slen        TYPE i,
         lv_result      TYPE TABLE OF text WITH HEADER LINE,
         lv_command(40) TYPE c.
*

  DATA v_idftp     TYPE zidftp.

* Get data FTP
  CLEAR : ps_zftp.
*
* Obtiene Datos FTP

  SELECT SINGLE rut
    FROM zta1_rutin
    INTO v_x25
   WHERE id EQ 'X25'.

    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.

      v_idftp = v_x25.

      SELECT SINGLE *
               INTO ps_zftp
               FROM zftp
*            WHERE zidftp = v_idftp.
              WHERE zidftp = v_idftp
                AND bukrs  = p_bukrs.

        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR ps_zftp.

        ELSE.

*  CHECK p_subrc IS INITIAL.
*
* Desactiva la revision extensa de errores.
*  SET EXTENDED CHECK OFF.
*  error = 0.
*
* Obtiene la longitud del campo de password
          lv_slen = strlen( ps_zftp-zpassw ).
*
* Funciön que encripta el password
          CALL FUNCTION 'HTTP_SCRAMBLE'
            EXPORTING
              source      = ps_zftp-zpassw
              sourcelen   = lv_slen
              key         = lv_key
            IMPORTING
              destination = ps_zftp-zpassw.
*
* Función que conecta el FTP del servidor de aplicación.
          CALL FUNCTION 'FTP_CONNECT'
            EXPORTING
              user            = ps_zftp-zuserftp
              password        = ps_zftp-zpassw
              host            = ps_zftp-zdirip
              rfc_destination = 'SAPFTPA'
            IMPORTING
              handle          = p_hdl.
*
* Función para ejecutar el comando de FTP
          lv_command = 'set passive on'.
          CALL FUNCTION 'FTP_COMMAND'
            EXPORTING
              handle        = p_hdl
              command       = lv_command
            TABLES
              data          = lv_result
            EXCEPTIONS
              tcpip_error   = 1
              command_error = 2
              data_error    = 3.
*
* Función para ejecutar el comando de FTP
          lv_command = 'ascii'.
          CALL FUNCTION 'FTP_COMMAND'
            EXPORTING
              handle        = p_hdl
              command       = lv_command       " DRUIZ 17.06.2014
            TABLES
              data          = lv_result
            EXCEPTIONS
              tcpip_error   = 1
              command_error = 2
              data_error    = 3.
*
* Si no se ejecuta correctamente el comando FTP
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            p_subrc = sy-subrc.
          ENDIF.
*--

        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_CONECTION_FTP
FORM f_disconnet_ftp  CHANGING p_hdl.
*--
  CALL FUNCTION 'FTP_DISCONNECT'
    EXPORTING
      handle = p_hdl.
*
  CLEAR p_hdl.
*--
ENDFORM.                    " F_DISCONNET_FTP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_UPLOAD_FILE_FTP_X
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LT_TABLE_BIN_RESP  text
*      -->P_LV_FILENAME  text
*      <--P_LV_SUBRC  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_upload_file_ftp_x TABLES pt_table_bin
                          USING p_hdl
                                p_path
                                pv_subrc TYPE sy-subrc
  CHANGING pv_length TYPE i.
*--
  DATA : lv_mode   TYPE c LENGTH 1 VALUE 'X'.
*         lv_length TYPE i.
*
  REFRESH pt_table_bin.

  IF pv_subrc = 0.

*  CLEAR p_subrc.
*
*  IF gv_times IS INITIAL.
*    gv_times = 1.
*  ENDIF.
*
*  DO gv_times TIMES.
*   Obtener archivo para cancelacion
    CALL FUNCTION 'FTP_SERVER_TO_R3'
      EXPORTING
        handle        = p_hdl
        fname         = p_path
      IMPORTING
        blob_length   = pv_length
      TABLES
        blob          = pt_table_bin
      EXCEPTIONS
        tcpip_error   = 1
        command_error = 2
        data_error    = 3
        OTHERS        = 4.
    IF pt_table_bin[] IS INITIAL.
*      WAIT UP TO 1 SECONDS.
*      p_subrc = '4'.
    ELSE.
*      CLEAR p_subrc.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
*  ENDDO.
*--

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_UPLOAD_FILE_FTP_X
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F_ADD_TC_FINAL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_LV_SUM_DIF  text
*      <--P_LV_SUM_MI  text
*      <--P_LWA_ZTA1_AUX_TC  text
*      <--P_LV_CONV  text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_add_tc_final
   CHANGING pv_sum_dif TYPE bseg-wrbtr
            pv_sum_mi  TYPE bseg-wrbtr
            pwa_zta1_aux_tc TYPE zsa1_cp1
            pv_conv TYPE c.

  DATA lwa_tc TYPE ty_tc. "TC VYGC
  DATA lv_tc(9) TYPE p DECIMALS 6.
  DATA lv_tc_str TYPE string.
  DATA lv_rest TYPE bseg-wrbtr.

*  IF pwa_zta1_aux_tc-ztmonp = c_mxn.

  lv_rest  =  pwa_zta1_aux_tc-ztpagobco - pv_sum_mi.

  IF lv_rest IS NOT INITIAL.

    lwa_tc-tc  =  pv_sum_dif / lv_rest.
*INI VYGC 21.05.2023
    lwa_tc-tc = lwa_tc-tc + '0.0000000001'.
*FIN VYGC 21.05.2023
    lv_tc_dr = lwa_tc-tc. "dnavoa 19.09.22 CP 2.0 (se necesitan 6 decimales)
*    lv_tc_dr = 1 / lwa_tc-tc. "dnavoa 28.02.23 CP 2.0 (se necesitan 10 decimales)
*  lwa_tc-tc  =  pv_sum_dif / ( pwa_zta1_aux_tc-ztpagobco - pv_sum_mi ).

  ENDIF.

  IF lwa_tc-tc IS NOT INITIAL.
    pwa_zta1_aux_tc-tc_dr = 1 / lwa_tc-tc.
    lv_tc_dr = lwa_tc-tc. "dnavoa 19.09.22 CP 2.0 (se necesitan 6 decimales)
*    lv_tc_dr = 1 / lwa_tc-tc. "dnavoa 28.02.23 CP 2.0 (se necesitan 10 decimales)

*  ELSE.
*
*    lv_tc = ( pwa_zta1_aux_tc-ztpagobco - pv_sum_mi ) / pv_sum_dif.

*  ENDIF.

*  lv_tc_str = lv_tc.
*  lwa_tc-tc = lv_tc_str+0(7).
    lwa_tc-bukrs = pwa_zta1_aux_tc-bukrs.
    lwa_tc-belnr = pwa_zta1_aux_tc-ztpago.
    lwa_tc-gjahr = pwa_zta1_aux_tc-anio_pago.

    APPEND lwa_tc TO t_tc.

    MODIFY t_zta1_aux FROM pwa_zta1_aux_tc TRANSPORTING tc_dr
                                           WHERE bukrs = pwa_zta1_aux_tc-bukrs AND
                                                 ztpago = pwa_zta1_aux_tc-ztpago AND
                                                 anio_pago = pwa_zta1_aux_tc-anio_pago.

  ENDIF.

  CLEAR: pwa_zta1_aux_tc,
         lwa_tc,
         pv_sum_dif,
         pv_sum_mi,
         pv_conv.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F_GET_TC_FINAL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_PV_SUM_DIF  text
*      <--P_PV_SUM_MI  text
*      <--P_PV_CONV  text
*      <--P_PWA_ZTA1_AUX_TC  text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_tc_final
CHANGING pv_sum_dif TYPE bseg-wrbtr
         pv_sum_mi TYPE bseg-wrbtr
         pv_conv TYPE c
         pwa_zta1_aux_tc TYPE zsa1_cp1.

  IF wa_zta1_aux-estatus = c_verde OR
     wa_zta1_aux-estatus = c_amarillo.

    IF wa_zta1_aux-ztmonp NE wa_zta1_aux-moneda_dr.
*    IF wa_zta1_aux-ztmonp = c_mxn.

*  sumar los usd
*      pv_sum_dif = pv_sum_dif + wa_zta1_aux-imp_pag.

*    ELSEIF wa_zta1_aux-ztmonp = c_usd.

*  sumar los mxn
      pv_sum_dif = pv_sum_dif + wa_zta1_aux-imp_pag.

*    ENDIF.

      pv_conv = c_x. "HAY CONVINACIONES DE MONEDAS

    ELSE.
      pv_sum_mi = pv_sum_mi + wa_zta1_aux-imp_pag.
    ENDIF.

*  dividir imp banco / suma de usd
*  dividir imp banco / suma de MXN
*W_TC-TC =  wa_zta1_aux-ztpagobco / v_sum.

    IF wa_zta1_aux-estatus = c_verde.
      pwa_zta1_aux_tc = wa_zta1_aux.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F_CATALOGO_IMPUESTOS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_catalogo_impuestos.

  DATA: lt_fieldcat_aux TYPE TABLE OF lvc_s_fcat,
        lwa_fieldcat    TYPE lvc_s_fcat,
        lv_cont         TYPE int4.

  lt_fieldcat_aux = t_fieldcat.

  SORT lt_fieldcat_aux BY col_pos DESCENDING.

  READ TABLE lt_fieldcat_aux INTO lwa_fieldcat
    INDEX 1.
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    lv_cont = lwa_fieldcat-col_pos.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.
  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'OBJIMPDR'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r01.
  lwa_fieldcat-lzero     = c_x.
  lwa_fieldcat-outputlen = 10.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.
  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'RPORCENTAJEIMPTO'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r08.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.
*************  IVA  ******************************

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.
  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'IVAIND_IMPTO'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r06.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.
  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'IVABASERETDR'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r09.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.
  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'IVAIMPTORETDR'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r10.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.
  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'IVATPFACTORRETDR'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r11.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.
  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'IVATASAOCUOTARETDR'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r07.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.
  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'IVAIMPTERETDR'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r12.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.
  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'IVABASEIMPDR'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r02.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.
  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'IVABASEIMPDRML'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r03.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.

  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.

*************  IVA  ******************************

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.

  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'ISRTPFACTORRETDR'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r33.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.
  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'ISRTASAOCUOTARETDR'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r29.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.
  lwa_fieldcat-col_pos = lv_cont.
  lwa_fieldcat-fieldname = 'ISRIMPTERETDR'.
  lwa_fieldcat-tabname   = 'ZSA1_CP1'.
  lwa_fieldcat-coltext = TEXT-r34.
  APPEND lwa_fieldcat TO t_fieldcat.

  CLEAR lwa_fieldcat.
  lv_cont = lv_cont + 1.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F_ACT_CP2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& -->  p1        text
*& <--  p2        text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_act_cp2.
  DATA: lwa_zta1_cp1 TYPE zta1_cp1,
        lwa_zta1_2   TYPE zsa1_cp1,
        lt_zta1_cp2  TYPE TABLE OF zta2_cp2,
        lwa_zta1_cp2 TYPE zta2_cp2.

  LOOP AT t_zta1_cp1_2 INTO lwa_zta1_cp1.

    READ TABLE t_zta1_2 INTO lwa_zta1_2
       WITH KEY seleccion = lwa_zta1_cp1-seleccion
                numcomp = lwa_zta1_cp1-numcomp
                bukrs = lwa_zta1_cp1-bukrs
                kunnr = lwa_zta1_cp1-kunnr
                ztpago = lwa_zta1_cp1-ztpago
                anio_pago = lwa_zta1_cp1-anio_pago
                ztfactrel = lwa_zta1_cp1-ztfactrel
                anio_fac = lwa_zta1_cp1-anio_fac.

    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.

      MOVE-CORRESPONDING lwa_zta1_2 TO lwa_zta1_cp2.

      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16ind_impto = lwa_zta1_2-16ind_impto.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16baseimptodr = lwa_zta1_2-16baseimptodr.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16impuestodr = lwa_zta1_2-16impuestodr.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16tpfactorimptodr = lwa_zta1_2-16tpfactorimptodr.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16tasaocuotaimptodr = lwa_zta1_2-16tasaocuotaimptodr.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16impteimptodr = lwa_zta1_2-16impteimptodr.
*      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16baseimpdr = lwa_zta1_2-16baseimpdr.
*      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16baseimpdrml = lwa_zta1_2-16baseimpdrml.
*      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16importeimpto = lwa_zta1_2-16importeimpto.
*      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16importeimptoml = lwa_zta1_2-16importeimptoml.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8ind_impto = lwa_zta1_2-8ind_impto.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8baseimptodr = lwa_zta1_2-8baseimptodr.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8impuestodr = lwa_zta1_2-8impuestodr.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8tpfactorimptodr = lwa_zta1_2-8tpfactorimptodr.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8tasaocuotaimptodr = lwa_zta1_2-8tasaocuotaimptodr.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8impteimptodr = lwa_zta1_2-8impteimptodr.
*      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8baseimpdr = lwa_zta1_2-8baseimpdr.
*      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8baseimpdrml = lwa_zta1_2-8baseimpdrml.
*      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8importeimpto = lwa_zta1_2-8importeimpto.
*      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8importeimptoml = lwa_zta1_2-8importeimptoml.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0ind_impto = lwa_zta1_2-0ind_impto.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0baseimptodr = lwa_zta1_2-0baseimptodr.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0impuestodr = lwa_zta1_2-0impuestodr.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0tpfactorimptodr = lwa_zta1_2-0tpfactorimptodr.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0tasaocuotaimptodr = lwa_zta1_2-0tasaocuotaimptodr.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0impteimptodr = lwa_zta1_2-0impteimptodr.
*      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0baseimpdr = lwa_zta1_2-0baseimpdr.
*      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0baseimpdrml = lwa_zta1_2-0baseimpdrml.
*      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0importeimpto = lwa_zta1_2-0importeimpto.
*      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0importeimptoml = lwa_zta1_2-0importeimptoml.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16baseimptop = lwa_zta1_2-16baseimptop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16impuestop = lwa_zta1_2-16impuestop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16tpfactorimptop = lwa_zta1_2-16tpfactorimptop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16tasaocuotaimptop = lwa_zta1_2-16tasaocuotaimptop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z16impteimptop = lwa_zta1_2-16impteimptop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8baseimptop = lwa_zta1_2-8baseimptop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8impuestop = lwa_zta1_2-8impuestop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8tpfactorimptop = lwa_zta1_2-8tpfactorimptop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8tasaocuotaimptop = lwa_zta1_2-8tasaocuotaimptop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z8impteimptop = lwa_zta1_2-8impteimptop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0baseimptop = lwa_zta1_2-0baseimptop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0impuestop = lwa_zta1_2-0impuestop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0tpfactorimptop = lwa_zta1_2-0tpfactorimptop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0tasaocuotaimptop = lwa_zta1_2-0tasaocuotaimptop.
      lwa_zta1_cp2-z0impteimptop = lwa_zta1_2-0impteimptop.

      APPEND lwa_zta1_cp2 TO lt_zta1_cp2.

    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

  MODIFY zta2_cp2 FROM TABLE lt_zta1_cp2.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form f_descargar_req
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_descargar_req
     USING lwa_zta1_2 TYPE zsa1_cp1.

  DATA: lt_xtab      TYPE lxe_x_tab_255.
  DATA: lv_message    TYPE string.
  DATA wa_zfit_filews_com TYPE zfit_filews_com.

  DATA : lv_path        TYPE zpath,
         lv_ruta        TYPE string,
         lv_file        TYPE filename,
         lv_filename    TYPE string,
         lv_xml_xstring TYPE xstring,
         lv_refdoc      TYPE c LENGTH 12.
*
  CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>directory_browse
    EXPORTING
      window_title    = 'ELECCIÓN DE RUTA DE DESCARGA XMLs'
      initial_folder  = 'C:\'
    CHANGING
      selected_folder = lv_ruta
    EXCEPTIONS
      cntl_error      = 1
      error_no_gui    = 2.
*
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0 AND lv_ruta IS NOT INITIAL.

    CONCATENATE lv_ruta '\' INTO lv_ruta.
*
    CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_OUTPUT'
      EXPORTING
        input  = lwa_zta1_2-numcomp
      IMPORTING
        output = lv_refdoc.

    SELECT SINGLE
           *
      FROM zfit_filews_com
      INTO wa_zfit_filews_com
     WHERE numcomp = lwa_zta1_2-numcomp
       AND bukrs = lwa_zta1_2-bukrs
       AND kunnr = lwa_zta1_2-kunnr
       AND ztpago = lwa_zta1_2-ztpago
       AND anio_pago = lwa_zta1_2-anio_pago.

      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.

        lv_xml_xstring = wa_zfit_filews_com-request.

        PERFORM f_convert_xstring_to_xtab USING lv_xml_xstring
                                       CHANGING lt_xtab.

*     Nombre Archivo
        CONCATENATE lv_refdoc
                    '_REQUEST'
                    '.xml'
               INTO lv_file.

        CALL FUNCTION 'HR_99S_DOWNLOAD'
          EXPORTING
            p_fsdialog          = abap_false
            p_initial_directory = lv_ruta
            p_filename          = lv_file
          TABLES
            data_tab            = lt_xtab.
*      ENDIF.

      ELSE.
        MESSAGE e000(zcfdi) INTO lv_message.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form f_convert_xstring_to_xtab
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      --> LV_XML_XSTRING
*&      <-- LT_XTAB
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_convert_xstring_to_xtab USING pv_xstring  TYPE xstring
                            CHANGING ct_xtab     TYPE lxe_x_tab_255.

  DATA lo_xml   TYPE REF TO cl_xml_document_base.
*
  CREATE OBJECT lo_xml.
*
  REFRESH ct_xtab.
*
  IF lo_xml->parse_xstring( stream = pv_xstring ) = 0.
    lo_xml->render_2_table( IMPORTING table = ct_xtab ).
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form f_descargar_res
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_descargar_res
     USING lwa_zta1_2 TYPE zsa1_cp1.

  DATA: lt_xtab      TYPE lxe_x_tab_255.
  DATA: lv_message    TYPE string.
  DATA: wa_zfit_filews_com TYPE zfit_filews_com.

  DATA : lv_path        TYPE zpath,
         lv_ruta        TYPE string,
         lv_file        TYPE filename,
         lv_filename    TYPE string,
         lv_xml_xstring TYPE xstring,
         lv_refdoc      TYPE c LENGTH 12.
*
  CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>directory_browse
    EXPORTING
      window_title    = 'ELECCIÓN DE RUTA DE DESCARGA XMLs'
      initial_folder  = 'C:\'
    CHANGING
      selected_folder = lv_ruta
    EXCEPTIONS
      cntl_error      = 1
      error_no_gui    = 2.
*
  IF sy-subrc EQ 0 AND lv_ruta IS NOT INITIAL.

    CONCATENATE lv_ruta '\' INTO lv_ruta.
*
    CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_OUTPUT'
      EXPORTING
        input  = lwa_zta1_2-numcomp
      IMPORTING
        output = lv_refdoc.

    SELECT SINGLE
           *
      FROM zfit_filews_com
      INTO wa_zfit_filews_com
     WHERE numcomp = lwa_zta1_2-numcomp
       AND bukrs = lwa_zta1_2-bukrs
       AND kunnr = lwa_zta1_2-kunnr
       AND ztpago = lwa_zta1_2-ztpago
       AND anio_pago = lwa_zta1_2-anio_pago.

      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.

        lv_xml_xstring = wa_zfit_filews_com-response.

        PERFORM f_convert_xstring_to_xtab USING lv_xml_xstring
                                       CHANGING lt_xtab.

*     Nombre Archivo
        CONCATENATE lv_refdoc
                    '_RESPONSE'
                    '.xml'
               INTO lv_file.

        CALL FUNCTION 'HR_99S_DOWNLOAD'
          EXPORTING
            p_fsdialog          = abap_false
            p_initial_directory = lv_ruta
            p_filename          = lv_file
          TABLES
            data_tab            = lt_xtab.
*      ENDIF.

      ELSE.
        MESSAGE e000(zcfdi) INTO lv_message.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WS_TIMBRA_CPAGO
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_T_XML  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM ws_timbra_cpago
        USING vxml
              vbukrs
              lv_name2  TYPE string
              pnumcomp TYPE znumcomp
              p_rfcemi TYPE stcd1
              p_rfcrec TYPE zcfdide016

        CHANGING
              po_logger TYPE REF TO zcl_logger
              lv_uuid          TYPE zfid_uuid_cfdi
              lv_fechatimbrado TYPE string
              lv_certificado   TYPE string
              lv_pathpdf       TYPE string
              lv_estatus       TYPE string
              lv_text_stat     TYPE string
              lv_xml_timbrado  TYPE xstring
              pwa_zcfdit002 TYPE zcfdit002
              lv_response     TYPE xstring
              pv_resstr        TYPE string.    " <--- NUEVO

  DATA:
    wa_sovos            TYPE zcp_sovos,
    wa_req_resp         TYPE zfit_filews_com,
    lv_version          TYPE zcfdide000,

    lv_sellocfd         TYPE zcfdide005,
    lv_sellosat         TYPE zcfdide006,
    lv_nocertificadosat TYPE zcfdide004,
    lv_cadena_org       TYPE string,
    lv_fechatimbradox   TYPE zcfdide003.

  DATA:
    p_rfcemix TYPE stcd1,
    p_rfcrecx TYPE zcfdide016.


  DATA: ws_timbrardes TYPE REF TO ztest_co_iemision,
        st_input_t    TYPE ztest_emitir_comprobante_reque,
        st_output_t   TYPE ztest_emitir_comprobante_respo,
        st_input_tp   TYPE ztest_reporte_procesamiento_d1,
        st_output_tp  TYPE ztest_reporte_procesamiento_de.
  DATA :
    lv_apikey TYPE zfe_apikey-apikey,
    e_error   TYPE string.
  DATA: ws_header TYPE REF TO if_wsprotocol_ws_header,
        name      TYPE string,
        namespace TYPE string,
        l_string  TYPE string,
        l_xstring TYPE xstring.
  DATA: ixml         TYPE REF TO if_ixml,
        xml_document TYPE REF TO if_ixml_document,
        xml_root     TYPE REF TO if_ixml_element,
        xml_element  TYPE REF TO if_ixml_element,
        xml_node     TYPE REF TO if_ixml_node.

  DATA: ws_timbrarpro TYPE REF TO zpro_co_iemision,
        st_input_p    TYPE zpro_emitir_comprobante_reques,
        ls_output     TYPE zpro_emitir_comprobante_respon.
  DATA: lcl_cx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.

  DATA: msg_error      TYPE string,
        p_sello_emisor TYPE string,
        p_sello_sat    TYPE string.
  CLEAR lv_apikey.

  DATA resstr TYPE string.
  DATA: st_property TYPE ztest_transaction_property.
  DATA:
    lv_buffer   TYPE xstring,
    gt_xml_data TYPE TABLE OF smum_xmltb,
    st_xml_data TYPE smum_xmltb,
    gt_return   TYPE TABLE OF bapiret2.



  DATA: lv_xml_timb TYPE xstring,
        result_xml  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF smum_xmltb,
        return      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapiret2,
        ls_xml      TYPE smum_xmltb,
        ls_return   TYPE bapiret2,
        lv_etiqueta TYPE c LENGTH 100.



  "Nueva tabla ZFE_APIKEY_ALL
  SELECT SINGLE apikey
    FROM zfe_apikey_all
    INTO lv_apikey
    WHERE bukrs = vbukrs.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CONCATENATE TEXT-007 vbukrs INTO e_error SEPARATED BY space.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.


    IF sy-sysid EQ 'TPP'. "Sistema productivo
    ELSE.
      DATA v_ruta2   TYPE string.
      DATA:
        it_properties_t TYPE TABLE OF ztest_transaction_property,
        st_properties_t TYPE ztest_transaction_property.

      st_input_t-comprobante = vxml."String xml

      st_input_tp-nombre_archivo = 'MiComplemento'.


      st_properties_t-key    = 'TipoCFD'.
      st_properties_t-value  = 'P'. "'PAGO'.

      APPEND st_properties_t TO it_properties_t.
      st_input_t-transaction_properties-transaction_property = it_properties_t.


      TRY.
          CREATE OBJECT ws_timbrardes
            EXPORTING
              logical_port_name = 'ZTEST_CO_IEMISION_PORT'.

          ws_header ?= ws_timbrardes->get_protocol('IF_WSPROTOCOL_WS_HEADER').
*additional header:
          CONCATENATE
                      '<soapenv:Header>'
                      '<cus:ApiKey  xmlns:cus="http://www.reachcore.com/CFDI/customHeaders">'
*                      '5tvxwkbcyaxhsfr6jyhzpydskrm6ntwawnxgavxe5ztfakp7dj6v8hntimctz74nxq5rprwtvsxd6mex'   "Api Key MGS
                      lv_apikey
                      '</cus:ApiKey>'
                      '</soapenv:Header>'
           INTO l_string.
*convert to xstring
          l_xstring = cl_proxy_service=>cstring2xstring( l_string ).

          IF NOT l_string IS INITIAL.
*create iXML DOM document from XML xstring
            CALL FUNCTION 'SDIXML_XML_TO_DOM'
              EXPORTING
                xml           = l_xstring
              IMPORTING
                document      = xml_document
              EXCEPTIONS
                invalid_input = 1
                OTHERS        = 2.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND NOT xml_document IS INITIAL.
              xml_root = xml_document->get_root_element( ).
              xml_element ?= xml_root->get_first_child( ).
*add header element by element to SOAP header
              WHILE NOT xml_element IS INITIAL.
                name = xml_element->get_name( ).
                namespace = xml_element->get_namespace_uri( ).
                IF NOT xml_element IS INITIAL.
                ENDIF.
                ws_header->set_request_header(
                            name = name
                            namespace = namespace
                            dom = xml_element ).
                xml_element ?= xml_element->get_next( ).
              ENDWHILE.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.



          CALL METHOD ws_timbrardes->emitir_comprobante
            EXPORTING
              emitir_comprobante_request  = st_input_t
            IMPORTING
              emitir_comprobante_response = st_output_t.
        CATCH cx_ai_system_fault INTO lcl_cx_root.
          msg_error = lcl_cx_root->get_text( ).
        CATCH cx_ai_application_fault INTO lcl_cx_root.
          msg_error = lcl_cx_root->get_text( ).
      ENDTRY.

      ls_output-error-inner_errors = st_output_t-error-inner_errors.
      ls_output-result = st_output_t-result.
      MOVE st_output_t-transaction_properties-transaction_property[] TO ls_output-transaction_properties-transaction_property[].

    ENDIF.

    "RECUPERO VALORES DEL PAC
    "SE GUARDA EN UNA TABLA LLAMADA ZFIT_FILEWS_COM
    "LOS DATOS SON PARA VISUALIZAR LOS BOTONES DE RESPONSE Y REQUEST.
    "==========================PAC============================

    PERFORM get_response_pac
                  USING

                        st_property
                  CHANGING
                        ls_output
                        lv_fechatimbrado
                        lv_certificado
                        lv_cadena_org
                        lv_pathpdf
                        lv_estatus
                        lv_text_stat
                        po_logger
                        resstr
                        .
    "==========================ERROR EN TIMBRADO============================
    "SI EXISTE ERROR EN TIMBRADO LO RECOLECTA
    IF NOT resstr IS INITIAL.
      pv_resstr = resstr.
*    PERFORM add_log_errores_pac
*              CHANGING
*                  po_logger
*                  resstr
*                  e_error.
* " Mismo comportamiento que éxito, misma carpeta/naming base
      PERFORM f_open_pac_resstr_xml_success
        USING resstr lv_name2 pnumcomp 'Error de timbrado (PAC)'.
    ELSE.
      "==========================ERROR EN TIMBRADO============================
      CALL FUNCTION 'SCMS_STRING_TO_XSTRING'
        EXPORTING
          text   = st_output_t-result
        IMPORTING
          buffer = lv_xml_timb
        EXCEPTIONS
          failed = 1
          OTHERS = 2.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        IF lv_xml_timb IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_response  = lv_xml_timb.
          PERFORM xml_descarga_timbrado USING lv_name2 ls_output-result.
          PERFORM save_read_xml_path USING lv_name2 ls_output-result.
          PERFORM f_parse_and_upsert_sovos
            USING    lv_xml_timb         " xstring del timbrado
                     vbukrs
                     pnumcomp
                     lv_cadena_org
                     ls_output-result
            CHANGING lv_version
                     lv_sellosat
                     lv_sellocfd
                     lv_nocertificadosat
                     p_rfcemix
                     p_rfcrecx
                     lv_fechatimbrado    " <- ahora compatible (string)
                     lv_uuid.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

FORM xml_descarga USING lv_name2.
  DATA v_ruta2 TYPE string.

  CONCATENATE 'C:\Facturas\' lv_name2 INTO v_ruta2.
  CALL FUNCTION 'GUI_DOWNLOAD'
    EXPORTING
      filename                = v_ruta2
      filetype                = 'ASC'
*     write_field_separator   = ','
    TABLES
      data_tab                = t_xml
    EXCEPTIONS
      file_write_error        = 1
      no_batch                = 2
      gui_refuse_filetransfer = 3
      invalid_type            = 4
      no_authority            = 5
      unknown_error           = 6
      header_not_allowed      = 7
      separator_not_allowed   = 8
      filesize_not_allowed    = 9
      header_too_long         = 10
      dp_error_create         = 11
      dp_error_send           = 12
      dp_error_write          = 13
      unknown_dp_error        = 14
      access_denied           = 15
      dp_out_of_memory        = 16
      disk_full               = 17
      dp_timeout              = 18
      file_not_found          = 19
      dataprovider_exception  = 20
      control_flush_error     = 21
      OTHERS                  = 22.
ENDFORM.
FORM xml_descarga_timbrado
  USING
    lv_name2  TYPE string
    i_string  TYPE string.

  DATA: lv_dir   TYPE string VALUE 'C:\Facturas',
        lv_file  TYPE string,
        lv_ok    TYPE abap_bool,
        lv_msg   TYPE string,
        lv_xstr  TYPE xstring,
        lv_exist TYPE abap_bool.

  " Evita llamadas GUI en background
  IF sy-batch = abap_true.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  " 1) Verificar si existe la carpeta
  TRY.
      lv_exist = cl_gui_frontend_services=>directory_exist(
                   directory = lv_dir ).
    CATCH cx_root.
      CLEAR lv_exist.
  ENDTRY.

  " 2) Crear la carpeta si no existe (vía FM portable)
  IF lv_exist = abap_false.
    CALL FUNCTION 'GUI_CREATE_DIRECTORY'
      EXPORTING
        directory = lv_dir
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS    = 1.
    " No interrumpimos si falla; quizá ya existe o no hay permisos
  ENDIF.

  " 3) Armar nombre de archivo y asegurar extensión .xml
  lv_file = |{ lv_dir }\\Timb_{ lv_name2 }|.
  PERFORM f_ensure_extension USING 'xml' CHANGING lv_file.

  " 4) Guardar contenido como UTF-8 binario
  PERFORM zconv_str_to_xstr USING i_string CHANGING lv_xstr.
  PERFORM f_xstr_save_bin   USING lv_xstr lv_file CHANGING lv_ok lv_msg.

  IF lv_ok IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE s000(00) WITH |No se pudo guardar XML: { lv_msg }| DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.



*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  SAVE_READ_XML_PATH
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LV_NAME2  text
*      -->P_LS_OUTPUT_RESULT  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM save_read_xml_path  USING
                              lv_name
                              i_string TYPE string.
  DATA v_ruta_server TYPE string.
  TYPES: BEGIN OF ty_text,
           line TYPE string,
         END OF ty_text.
  DATA: ti_text TYPE TABLE OF ty_text,
        st_text TYPE ty_text.

  st_text-line = i_string.
  APPEND st_text TO ti_text.

  CONCATENATE '/usr/sap/CPQAS_0100/' 'Timb_' lv_name INTO v_ruta_server.
  OPEN DATASET v_ruta_server FOR OUTPUT IN TEXT MODE ENCODING DEFAULT.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
*      loop at ti_text into Data(wa_text).
    TRANSFER i_string TO v_ruta_server.
    CLOSE DATASET v_ruta_server.
*      ENDLOOP.

  ENDIF.



ENDFORM.
FORM gui_download USING i_xstring TYPE xstring
                        i_fullpath TYPE string
               CHANGING c_subrc TYPE sy-subrc.
* Stores a XSTRING as file on frontend client

  DATA: lt_xtable   TYPE faa_t_tenv_xmlraw.
  DATA: ld_filesize TYPE i.

* Converting XString to XTable
  CALL METHOD cl_faa_tenv_services=>convert_xstring_to_xtable
    EXPORTING
      id_xstring    = i_xstring
    IMPORTING
      et_xtable     = lt_xtable
    EXCEPTIONS
      invalid_table = 1
      OTHERS        = 2.

  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    c_subrc = sy-subrc.
    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE 'I' NUMBER sy-msgno
               WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

* . Save XTable
  ld_filesize = xstrlen( i_xstring ).
  CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>gui_download
    EXPORTING
      bin_filesize = ld_filesize
      filename     = i_fullpath
      filetype     = 'BIN'
    CHANGING
      data_tab     = lt_xtable
    EXCEPTIONS
      OTHERS       = 4.
  c_subrc = sy-subrc.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE 'I' NUMBER sy-msgno
               WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    "gui_download
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_FILE_TIMBRADO_SAT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LC_VISXML  text
*      -->P_LWA_ZTA1_2_NUMCOMP  text
*      <--P_C_SUBRC  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_file_timbrado_sat  USING    p_type
                                     p_numcomp.

  DATA: gv_filename TYPE string,
        gv_path     TYPE string,
        gv_result   TYPE i,
        p_path      TYPE string,
        lv_xsrting  TYPE xstringval,
        lv_name     TYPE string,
        uuid        TYPE string.

  DATA: ob_ixml                TYPE REF TO if_ixml,
        ob_ixml_streamfactory  TYPE REF TO if_ixml_stream_factory,
        ob_ixml_istream        TYPE REF TO if_ixml_istream,
        ob_ixml_document       TYPE REF TO if_ixml_document,
        ob_parser              TYPE REF TO if_ixml_parser,
        ob_ixml_stream_factory TYPE REF TO if_ixml_stream_factory,
        ob_ixml_ostream        TYPE REF TO if_ixml_ostream,
        debug                  TYPE string,
        c_subrc                TYPE sy-subrc.

  CONSTANTS:
    c_vxml TYPE string VALUE 'xml',
    c_vpdf TYPE string VALUE 'pdf'.

  SELECT
     SINGLE xml_timb uuid_comp
  FROM
     zta1_cp1 INTO ( lv_xsrting , uuid )
  WHERE
    numcomp = p_numcomp.


    IF p_type  = 'VIS_XML'.
      CONCATENATE p_numcomp '.' c_vxml INTO lv_name.
      IF lv_xsrting IS NOT INITIAL.
        ob_ixml = cl_ixml=>create( ).
        ob_ixml_streamfactory = ob_ixml->create_stream_factory( ).
        ob_ixml_istream  = ob_ixml_streamfactory->create_istream_xstring( string = lv_xsrting ).
        ob_ixml_document = ob_ixml->create_document( ) .
        ob_parser = ob_ixml->create_parser( stream_factory = ob_ixml_streamfactory
                                            istream        = ob_ixml_istream
                                            document       = ob_ixml_document ).
        ob_parser->parse( ).

*              For debug purpose only
        ob_ixml_stream_factory = ob_ixml->create_stream_factory( ).
        ob_ixml_ostream = ob_ixml_stream_factory->create_ostream_cstring( debug ).
        ob_ixml_ostream->set_pretty_print( abap_true ).
        ob_ixml_document->render( ostream = ob_ixml_ostream ) .


*               PERFORM descargar_xml
*                         USING
*                            ppath "Ruta donde se descargo local
*                            debug "String del XML
*                            pfactura."Numero de Factura

        CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>file_save_dialog
          EXPORTING
*           window_title      = ‘ ‘
            default_extension = c_vxml
            default_file_name = lv_name
            initial_directory = 'C:'
          CHANGING
            filename          = gv_filename
            path              = gv_path
            fullpath          = p_path
            user_action       = gv_result.
        "Donwload file
        PERFORM gui_download USING lv_xsrting p_path CHANGING  c_subrc.


      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF p_type  = 'VIS_PDF'.
      CONCATENATE p_numcomp '.' c_vpdf INTO lv_name.

      CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>file_save_dialog
        EXPORTING
*         window_title      = ‘ ‘
          default_extension = c_vxml
          default_file_name = lv_name
          initial_directory = 'C:'
        CHANGING
          filename          = gv_filename
          path              = gv_path
          fullpath          = p_path
          user_action       = gv_result.
      "Donwload file
      PERFORM gui_download_pdf USING uuid p_path gv_filename CHANGING  c_subrc.

    ENDIF.



ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GUI_DOWNLOAD_PDF
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LV_XSRTING  text
*      -->P_P_PATH  text
*      <--P_C_SUBRC  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM gui_download_pdf  USING    p_uuid
                                p_path
                                gv_filename
                       CHANGING p_c_subrc.

  DATA :
    lv_url      TYPE string VALUE 'https://oat.reachcore.com/api/rest/Timbre/Get?uuid=0F9AED29-C5CD-4C51-8E13-C116A5C9011C&format=pdf',
    lo_client   TYPE REF TO if_http_client,
    lv_response TYPE xstring,
    lv_gurl     TYPE string,
    lv_filesize TYPE i,
    c_subrc     TYPE sy-subrc,
    lt_line     TYPE TABLE OF tline,
    data_tab    TYPE TABLE OF x255.

  CONCATENATE
        'https://oat.reachcore.com/api/rest/Timbre/Get?uuid='
        p_uuid
        '&format=pdf'
        INTO
          lv_url.


  cl_http_client=>create_by_url( EXPORTING url = lv_url  IMPORTING client = lo_client  ).
  IF lo_client IS BOUND.

    TRY.
*     Set request parameters
        lo_client->request->set_method( 'GET' ).
        lo_client->request->set_header_field( name = 'ApiKey' value = 'd3pgkvhhwdeatw7ec3by4zfjrtu99gzgkpzj8asgvhuwmkgvnnczdfjyhmx3fx6rbtygvuxqrgyfe2hv' ).


*      * Send the request
        lo_client->send( ).

*      * Get the response
        lo_client->receive( ).
        lv_response = lo_client->response->get_data( ).

        CALL FUNCTION 'SCMS_XSTRING_TO_BINARY'
          EXPORTING
            buffer     = lv_response
          TABLES
            binary_tab = data_tab.


        cl_gui_frontend_services=>gui_download(
            EXPORTING
                  filename = p_path
                  filetype = 'BIN'
            CHANGING
                  data_tab = data_tab ).

        cl_gui_frontend_services=>execute(
            EXPORTING
                  document = gv_filename ).

      CATCH cx_root INTO DATA(lx_root).
        WRITE: / 'Error:', lx_root->get_text( ).
    ENDTRY.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_TIMBRE_SOVOS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LV_XML_TIMBRADO  text
*      <--P_LV_SELLOCFD  text
*      <--P_LV_SELLOSAT  text
*      <--P_LV_CADENA_ORG  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_timbre_sovos  USING    p_lv_xml_timbrado
                                p_lv_rfcemi
                                p_lv_rfcrec
                       CHANGING p_lv_version
                                p_lv_sellosat
                                p_lv_sellocfd
                                p_lv_nocertificadosat
                                p_lv_rfcemix
                                p_lv_rfcrecx
                                p_lv_fechatimbrado
                                p_lv_uuid.
  DATA:
    ob_ixml                TYPE REF TO if_ixml,
    ob_ixml_streamfactory  TYPE REF TO if_ixml_stream_factory,
    lv_xsrting             TYPE xstringval,
    ob_ixml_istream        TYPE REF TO if_ixml_istream,
    ob_ixml_document       TYPE REF TO if_ixml_document,
    ob_parser              TYPE REF TO if_ixml_parser,
    ob_ixml_stream_factory TYPE REF TO if_ixml_stream_factory,
    ob_ixml_ostream        TYPE REF TO if_ixml_ostream,
    debug                  TYPE string,
    wa_xmldata_bin         TYPE xstring,
    result_xml             TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF smum_xmltb,
    return                 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapiret2,
    ls_xml                 TYPE smum_xmltb,
    cvalor                 TYPE string,
    p_emi                  TYPE i,
    p_rec                  TYPE i.

  IF p_lv_xml_timbrado IS NOT INITIAL.
    ob_ixml = cl_ixml=>create( ).
    ob_ixml_streamfactory = ob_ixml->create_stream_factory( ).
    ob_ixml_istream  = ob_ixml_streamfactory->create_istream_xstring( string = p_lv_xml_timbrado ).
    ob_ixml_document = ob_ixml->create_document( ) .
    ob_parser = ob_ixml->create_parser( stream_factory = ob_ixml_streamfactory
                                        istream        = ob_ixml_istream
                                        document       = ob_ixml_document ).
    ob_parser->parse( ).

*     For debug purpose only
    ob_ixml_stream_factory = ob_ixml->create_stream_factory( ).
    ob_ixml_ostream = ob_ixml_stream_factory->create_ostream_cstring( debug ).
    ob_ixml_ostream->set_pretty_print( abap_true ).
    ob_ixml_document->render( ostream = ob_ixml_ostream ) .

    CALL FUNCTION 'SCMS_STRING_TO_XSTRING'
      EXPORTING
        text   = debug          "variable type string
      IMPORTING
        buffer = wa_xmldata_bin.  " variable type xstring

    CALL FUNCTION 'SMUM_XML_PARSE'
      EXPORTING
        xml_input = wa_xmldata_bin
      TABLES
        xml_table = result_xml
        return    = return.

    LOOP AT result_xml INTO ls_xml.
      CASE ls_xml-cname.
        WHEN 'Version'.
          p_lv_version = ls_xml-cvalue.
        WHEN 'SelloCFD'.
          p_lv_sellocfd = ls_xml-cvalue.
        WHEN 'NoCertificadoSAT'.
          p_lv_nocertificadosat = ls_xml-cvalue.
        WHEN 'SelloSAT'.
          p_lv_sellosat = ls_xml-cvalue.
*          WHEN 'Rfc'.
*            p_lv_rfcrec = ls_xml-cvalue.
        WHEN 'Fecha'.
          p_lv_fechatimbrado = ls_xml-cvalue.

        WHEN 'UUID'.
          p_lv_uuid = ls_xml-cvalue.
      ENDCASE.
*        MODIFY zcfdi_head_40 FROM ls_head.
    ENDLOOP.

    LOOP AT result_xml INTO ls_xml WHERE hier = 2.

      CASE ls_xml-cname.
        WHEN 'Emisor'.
          p_emi = 1.
          p_rec = 0.
        WHEN 'Receptor'.
          p_emi = 0.
          p_rec = 1.
        WHEN 'Rfc'.
          IF p_emi = 1.
            p_lv_rfcemix = ls_xml-cvalue.
          ENDIF.
          IF p_rec = 1.
            p_lv_rfcrecx = ls_xml-cvalue.
          ENDIF.

      ENDCASE.
*        MODIFY zcfdi_head_40 FROM ls_head.
    ENDLOOP.


  ENDIF.



ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_ACTUALIZA_UUID
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_P_BELNR  text
*      -->P_P_GJAHR  text
*      -->P_VBUKRS  text
*      -->P_LV_UUID  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_actualiza_uuid  USING    p_p_belnr
                                p_p_gjahr
                                p_vbukrs
                                p_lv_uuid.

  DATA: lw_header    LIKE  thead,
        lw_lines     LIKE  tline,
        lt_lines     TYPE TABLE OF tline,
        lv_documento TYPE tdobname,
        lv_funcion   TYPE c.


  CONCATENATE p_vbukrs
              p_p_belnr
              p_p_gjahr
         INTO lv_documento.
  CONDENSE lv_documento.

  lw_header-tdobject = 'BELEG'.
  lw_header-tdname   = lv_documento.
  lw_header-tdid     = '0004'.
  lw_header-tdspras  = sy-langu.


  lw_lines-tdformat = '*'.
  lw_lines-tdline   = p_lv_uuid.
  APPEND lw_lines TO lt_lines.


  CALL FUNCTION 'SAVE_TEXT'
    EXPORTING
      client          = sy-mandt
      header          = lw_header
      insert          = 'X'
      savemode_direct = 'X'
    IMPORTING
      function        = lv_funcion
    TABLES
      lines           = lt_lines
    EXCEPTIONS
      id              = 1
      language        = 2
      name            = 3
      object          = 4
      OTHERS          = 5.


  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILE_ADJUNTAR_POLIZA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LWA_CLIENTES_NUMCOMP  text
*      -->P_LWA_CLIENTES_NUMCOMP  text
*      -->P_3622   text
*      -->P_3623   text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM file_adjuntar_poliza
  USING    lwa_zta1_2       TYPE zsa1_cp1
           p_numcomp        TYPE swo_typeid
           p_name           TYPE string
           p_descripcion    TYPE so_obj_des
           p_servidor       TYPE c
           p_string_xml     TYPE string
           p_key            TYPE swo_typeid
  CHANGING
           po_logger        TYPE REF TO zcl_logger.

  DATA: lt_pdf TYPE solix_tab.

  " Obtener el PDF desde spool o ALV
  PERFORM f_get_pdf_poliza
    USING    lwa_zta1_2
             p_numcomp
             p_name
    CHANGING lt_pdf.

  " Anexar PDF si existe contenido
  IF lt_pdf IS NOT INITIAL.
    CONCATENATE p_name '.pdf' INTO p_name.
    CALL FUNCTION 'ZAGREGA_FILE_FB03'
      EXPORTING
        p_pago       = p_numcomp
        archivo      = p_name
        descripcion  = p_descripcion
        servidor     = p_servidor
        p_xml        = ''
        p_pdf        = 'X'
        p_string_xml = ''
        p_key        = p_key
      TABLES
        it_pdf       = lt_pdf
      CHANGING
        po_logger    = po_logger.
  ENDIF.

  " Anexar XML si existe contenido
*  IF p_string_xml IS NOT INITIAL.
  DATA t_xmlx   TYPE zcp_sovos OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM zcp_sovos INTO t_xmlx WHERE numcomp = lwa_zta1_2-numcomp.
    CALL FUNCTION 'ZAGREGA_FILE_FB03'
      EXPORTING
        p_pago       = p_numcomp
        archivo      = p_name
        descripcion  = p_descripcion
        servidor     = p_servidor
        p_xml        = 'X'
        p_pdf        = ''
        p_string_xml = t_xmlx-str_xml
        p_key        = p_key
      TABLES
        it_pdf       = lt_pdf
      CHANGING
        po_logger    = po_logger.
*  ENDIF.

    " Log si no hay nada que anexar
    IF lt_pdf IS INITIAL .
      IF po_logger IS BOUND.
        CALL METHOD po_logger->add_message
          EXPORTING
            iv_msgty = 'I'
            iv_msgid = 'ZCFDI_40'
            iv_msgno = 11
            iv_msgv1 = 'No se encontró contenido para anexar (PDF/XML)'.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

ENDFORM.


*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_PDF_POLIZA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LWA_ZTA1_2  text
*      -->P_P_NUMCOMP  text
*      -->P_P_NAME  text
*      <--P_P_IT_PDF  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_pdf_poliza  USING    p_lwa_zta1_2 TYPE zsa1_cp1
                                p_p_numcomp
                                p_p_name
                       CHANGING p_p_it_pdf TYPE solix_tab.

  DATA: lc_regimen601(3)      TYPE c,
        lv_formname           TYPE tdsfname VALUE 'ZFISF_DOCTIMB3_VIKO',
        lwa_control_param     TYPE ssfctrlop,
        lwa_composer_param    TYPE ssfcompop,
        lwa_otfdata           TYPE ssfcrescl, " Smart Forms: Return value at end of form
        lv_band               TYPE c VALUE '',
        lv_fm_name            TYPE rs38l_fnam,
        lt_pagos              TYPE ztta1_cp1,
        lt_fac                TYPE ztta1_cp1,
        lt_otf                TYPE tsfotf,
        lv_reg_fis            TYPE char5,
        lv_pais               TYPE t005-intca3,
        lv_regimen            TYPE char3,
        lv_otfdata            TYPE TABLE OF itcoo,
        lv_pdf_file           TYPE string,
        lv_length             TYPE i,
        ls_tvarvc             TYPE tvarvc,
        v_bin_filesize        TYPE i,
        ls_print_data_to_read TYPE lbbil_print_data_to_read,
        cf_retcode            TYPE sy-subrc,
        ls_recipient          TYPE swotobjid,
        ls_sender             TYPE swotobjid,
        et_otf                TYPE TABLE OF itcoo WITH HEADER LINE,
        it_docs               TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF docs,
        it_lines              TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF tline,
        gv_path               TYPE string,
        p_path                TYPE string,
        lv_uuid               TYPE zfid_uuid_cfdi,
        lv_ztfetimb           TYPE zfid_fe_tim,
        lv_dom                TYPE pstlz,
        lv_rfc_emisor         TYPE paval,
        lt_zcp_sovos          TYPE TABLE OF zcp_sovos.



  SORT t_kna1
    BY kunnr.
*     lt_clientes[] = t_zta1_2[].
*      lt_pagos[]    = t_zta1_2[].
*      lt_fac[]      = t_zta1_2[].

**********************************************************************
* Recupero datos SD.

  PERFORM f_datos_sd.

* Recupero el nombre de Archivo Parametrizado en la Transacción STVARV.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM tvarvc INTO ls_tvarvc WHERE name = 'CFDI40CP_PDF'.

    lv_formname = ls_tvarvc-low.

    PERFORM set_print_data_to_read USING    lv_formname
                                     CHANGING ls_print_data_to_read
                                     cf_retcode.


    PERFORM set_print_param
                          CHANGING lwa_control_param
                                   lwa_composer_param
                                   ls_recipient
                                   ls_sender
                                   cf_retcode.
**********************************************************************
    SELECT
      *
      INTO TABLE lt_zcp_sovos
    FROM
        zcp_sovos
    WHERE
        bukrs = p_lwa_zta1_2-bukrs
      AND numcomp = p_lwa_zta1_2-numcomp.

      LOOP AT lt_zcp_sovos INTO wa_zcp_sovos.
        " Aquí no es necesario usar DATA(ls_zcp_sovos), ya que estamos usando wa_zcp_sovos como WORK_AREA
        wa_zcp_sovos-mandt               = wa_zcp_sovos-mandt.
        wa_zcp_sovos-bukrs               = wa_zcp_sovos-bukrs.
        wa_zcp_sovos-numcomp             = wa_zcp_sovos-numcomp.
        wa_zcp_sovos-rfcemisor           = wa_zcp_sovos-rfcemisor.
        wa_zcp_sovos-rfcreceptor         = wa_zcp_sovos-rfcreceptor.
        wa_zcp_sovos-version             = wa_zcp_sovos-version.
        wa_zcp_sovos-uuid                = wa_zcp_sovos-uuid.
        wa_zcp_sovos-fechatimbrado       = wa_zcp_sovos-fechatimbrado.
        wa_zcp_sovos-nocertificadosat    = wa_zcp_sovos-nocertificadosat.
        wa_zcp_sovos-sellocfd            = wa_zcp_sovos-sellocfd.
        wa_zcp_sovos-sellosat            = wa_zcp_sovos-sellosat.
        wa_zcp_sovos-total_compr         = wa_zcp_sovos-total_compr.
        wa_zcp_sovos-code_qr             = wa_zcp_sovos-code_qr.
        wa_zcp_sovos-annul               = wa_zcp_sovos-annul.
        wa_zcp_sovos-nocertificadocsd    = wa_zcp_sovos-nocertificadocsd.
        wa_zcp_sovos-cadena_original     = wa_zcp_sovos-cadena_original.
      ENDLOOP.



      SELECT
          *
        INTO TABLE
        lt_pagos
      FROM  zta1_cp1
      WHERE
            numcomp =  p_lwa_zta1_2-numcomp.
        SELECT
            *
          INTO TABLE
          lt_fac
        FROM  zta1_cp1
        WHERE
              numcomp =  p_lwa_zta1_2-numcomp.

          READ TABLE lt_pagos INDEX 1 INTO DATA(lv_pago).
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            lv_uuid     = lv_pago-uuid_comp.
            lv_ztfetimb = lv_pago-ztfetimb.
            lv_rfc_emisor = lv_pago-rfc_cl.
          ENDIF.
          READ TABLE t_t001
            INTO DATA(pwa_t001)
            WITH KEY bukrs =  p_lwa_zta1_2-bukrs.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.

            READ TABLE t_adrc
                  INTO DATA(pwa_adrc)
              WITH KEY addrnumber = pwa_t001-adrnr
                BINARY SEARCH.
            IF sy-subrc NE 0.

            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.


*  lv_reg_fis = lwa_fact-regimen_fiscal.
*  WRITE lwa_fact-regimen_fiscal TO lv_reg_fis.
          WRITE '601' TO lv_reg_fis.
*  lv_pais    = pwa_factura_010-land1_sat.



*Determina la función del smartform
          CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
            EXPORTING
              formname           = lv_formname
            IMPORTING
              fm_name            = lv_fm_name
            EXCEPTIONS
              no_form            = 1
              no_function_module = 2
              OTHERS             = 3.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.

            MESSAGE ID sy-msgid
                  TYPE sy-msgty
                NUMBER sy-msgno
                  WITH sy-msgv1
                       sy-msgv2
                       sy-msgv3
                       sy-msgv4.
          ELSE.


*Llamar smartform
            CALL FUNCTION lv_fm_name
              EXPORTING
                archive_index      = toa_dara
*               archive_parameters = pwa_t001z
                control_parameters = lwa_control_param
                output_options     = lwa_composer_param
                user_settings      = space
                pwa_clientes       = p_lwa_zta1_2
                p_rfc_receptor     = p_lwa_zta1_2-rfc_cl   "pwa_kna1-stcd1
                p_nom_receptor     = p_lwa_zta1_2-nombre "pwa_kna1-name1
                p_rfc_emisor       = lv_rfc_emisor
                p_nom_emisor       = pwa_t001-butxt "pwa_t880-name1
                p_reg_fis          = lv_reg_fis
                p_reg_fisr         = lv_regimen
                p_dom_fis          = lv_dom   "LS 04.08.2022
                p_res_fis          = lv_pais "pwa_t005-intca3  "
                p_lug_exp          = pwa_adrc-post_code1
                pwa_zcfdit002      = wa_zcfdit002 "pwa_zcfdit002
                p_sust             = lv_band
                p_uuide            = lv_uuid
                p_fechat           = lv_ztfetimb
                pwa_zcp_sovos      = wa_zcp_sovos
                p_cadena_org       = wa_zcp_sovos-cadena_original
              IMPORTING
                job_output_info    = lwa_otfdata
              TABLES
                pt_pagos           = lt_pagos
                pt_fac             = lt_fac
                pt_fac2            = t_zta1_aux
*               pt_zcp_sovos       = lt_zcp_sovos
              EXCEPTIONS
                formatting_error   = 1
                internal_error     = 2
                send_error         = 3
                user_canceled      = 4
                OTHERS             = 5.

            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              lv_otfdata[] = lwa_otfdata-otfdata[].
              " Ahora convertimos OTF a PDF
*              CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERT_OTF'
*                EXPORTING
*                  format      = 'PDF'
*                  max_linewidth = 132
*                IMPORTING
*                  pdf_data    = lv_otfdata
*                EXCEPTIONS
*                  OTHERS      = 1.

              CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERT_OTF_2_PDF'
                IMPORTING
                  bin_filesize           = v_bin_filesize
                TABLES
                  otf                    = lv_otfdata
                  doctab_archive         = it_docs
                  lines                  = it_lines
                EXCEPTIONS
                  err_conv_not_possible  = 1
                  err_otf_mc_noendmarker = 2
                  OTHERS                 = 3.

*            lv_fname =  p_lwa_zta1_2-NUMCOMP.

              IF sy-subrc = 0.
                " La conversión fue exitosa, GENERA EL PDF
                DATA: gv_attachment TYPE solix_tab.
                DATA: e_binfile TYPE xstring.

                CLEAR p_p_it_pdf. REFRESH p_p_it_pdf.

                CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERT_OTF'
                  EXPORTING
                    format                = 'PDF'
                    max_linewidth         = 132
                  IMPORTING
                    bin_filesize          = v_bin_filesize
                    bin_file              = e_binfile
                  TABLES
                    otf                   = lv_otfdata
                    lines                 = it_lines
                  EXCEPTIONS
                    err_max_linewidth     = 1
                    err_format            = 2
                    err_conv_not_possible = 3
                    OTHERS                = 4.

                gv_attachment = cl_document_bcs=>xstring_to_solix( ip_xstring = e_binfile ).
                p_p_it_pdf[] = gv_attachment[].
              ELSE.
                WRITE: / 'Error al convertir OTF a PDF'.
              ENDIF.


            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_RESPONSE_PAC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_LS_OUTPUT  text
*      <--P_LV_FECHATIMBRADO  text
*      <--P_LV_CERTIFICADO  text
*      <--P_LV_CADENA_ORG  text
*      <--P_LV_PATHPDF  text
*      <--P_IF  text
*      <--P_LS_OUTPUT_ERROR_INNER_ERRORS  text
*      <--P_IS  text
*      <--P_NOT  text
*      <--P_INITIAL  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_response_pac  USING

                                st_property TYPE ztest_transaction_property
                       CHANGING
                                p_ls_output TYPE zpro_emitir_comprobante_respon
                                p_lv_fechatimbrado
                                p_lv_certificado
                                p_lv_cadena_org
                                p_lv_pathpdf
                                p_lv_estatus
                                p_lv_text_stat
                                po_logger TYPE REF TO zcl_logger
                                p_resstr    TYPE string.

  "Si existe error , return

  IF p_ls_output-error-inner_errors IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL FUNCTION 'ECATT_CONV_XSTRING_TO_STRING'
      EXPORTING
        im_xstring  = p_ls_output-error-inner_errors
        im_encoding = 'UTF-8'
      IMPORTING
        ex_string   = p_resstr.

    p_lv_estatus = c_rojo.
    p_lv_text_stat = TEXT-m08.

  ELSE.
    " si es Exito el Timbrado Envia los datos al LOG.

    p_lv_estatus = c_verde.
    p_lv_text_stat = TEXT-m07.
    "Timbre.FechaTimbrado
    READ TABLE p_ls_output-transaction_properties-transaction_property
    INTO st_property
    WITH KEY key = 'Timbre.FechaTimbrado'.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      p_lv_fechatimbrado = st_property-value.
      CALL METHOD po_logger->add_message
        EXPORTING
          iv_msgty = 'I'
          iv_msgid = 'ZCFDI_40'
          iv_msgno = '000'
          iv_msgv1 = 'Timbre.FechaTimbrado'
          iv_msgv2 = st_property-value.
    ENDIF.

    "Timbre.NoCertificadoSAT
    READ TABLE p_ls_output-transaction_properties-transaction_property
   INTO st_property
   WITH KEY key = 'Timbre.NoCertificadoSAT'.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      p_lv_certificado = st_property-value.
      CALL METHOD po_logger->add_message
        EXPORTING
          iv_msgty = 'I'
          iv_msgid = 'ZCFDI_40'
          iv_msgno = '000'
          iv_msgv1 = 'Timbre.NoCertificadoSAT'
          iv_msgv2 = st_property-value.
    ENDIF.
    "Timbre.CadenaOriginal
    READ TABLE p_ls_output-transaction_properties-transaction_property
   INTO st_property
   WITH KEY key = 'Timbre.CadenaOriginal'.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      p_lv_cadena_org = st_property-value.
      CALL METHOD po_logger->add_message
        EXPORTING
          iv_msgty = 'I'
          iv_msgid = 'ZCFDI_40'
          iv_msgno = '000'
          iv_msgv1 = 'Timbre.CadenaOriginal'
          iv_msgv2 = st_property-value.
    ENDIF.


    "RESULT.PdfUrl
    READ TABLE p_ls_output-transaction_properties-transaction_property
   INTO st_property
   WITH KEY key = 'RESULT.PdfUrl'.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      p_lv_pathpdf = st_property-value.
      CALL METHOD po_logger->add_message
        EXPORTING
          iv_msgty = 'I'
          iv_msgid = 'ZCFDI_40'
          iv_msgno = '000'
          iv_msgv1 = 'RESULT.PdfUrl'
          iv_msgv2 = st_property-value.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ADD_LOG_ERRORES_PAC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_PO_LOGGER  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM add_log_errores_pac
              CHANGING
                    po_logger TYPE REF TO zcl_logger
                    resstr TYPE string
                    e_error.

  DATA:
    lv_buffer   TYPE xstring,
    gt_xml_data TYPE TABLE OF smum_xmltb,
    gt_return   TYPE TABLE OF bapiret2,
    st_xml_data TYPE smum_xmltb.

  CLEAR lv_buffer.
  CALL FUNCTION 'SCMS_STRING_TO_XSTRING'
    EXPORTING
      text   = resstr
    IMPORTING
      buffer = lv_buffer
    EXCEPTIONS
      failed = 1
      OTHERS = 2.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
  ENDIF.
  CALL FUNCTION 'SMUM_XML_PARSE'
    EXPORTING
      xml_input = lv_buffer
    TABLES
      xml_table = gt_xml_data
      return    = gt_return.
  DATA ipaso TYPE i VALUE 0.
  LOOP AT gt_xml_data INTO st_xml_data.
    ipaso = 0.
    TRANSLATE st_xml_data-cname TO UPPER CASE.
    IF st_xml_data-cname EQ 'DETAILS'.
      e_error = st_xml_data-cvalue.
      ipaso = 1.
      CALL METHOD po_logger->add_message
        EXPORTING
          iv_msgty = 'E'
          iv_msgid = 'ZCFDI_40'
          iv_msgno = '001'
          iv_msgv1 = st_xml_data-cname
          iv_msgv2 = st_xml_data-cvalue.
*              iv_msgv3 =
*              iv_msgv4 = .
    ELSEIF st_xml_data-cname EQ 'SELLOCFD'.
*      ls_head-sello_cfd = st_xml_data-cvalue.
      ipaso = 2.
      CALL METHOD po_logger->add_message
        EXPORTING
          iv_msgty = 'E'
          iv_msgid = 'ZCFDI_40'
          iv_msgno = '001'
          iv_msgv1 = st_xml_data-cname
          iv_msgv2 = st_xml_data-cvalue.
*              iv_msgv3 =
*              iv_msgv4 = .
    ELSEIF st_xml_data-cname EQ 'SELLOSAT'.
*      ls_head-sello_sat = st_xml_data-cvalue.
      ipaso = 3.
      CALL METHOD po_logger->add_message
        EXPORTING
          iv_msgty = 'E'
          iv_msgid = 'ZCFDI_40'
          iv_msgno = '000'
          iv_msgv1 = st_xml_data-cname
          iv_msgv2 = st_xml_data-cvalue.
*              iv_msgv3 =
*              iv_msgv4 = .
    ENDIF.

    IF ipaso = 0.

      CALL METHOD po_logger->add_message
        EXPORTING
          iv_msgty = 'E'
          iv_msgid = 'ZCFDI_40'
          iv_msgno = '000'
          iv_msgv1 = st_xml_data-cname
          iv_msgv2 = st_xml_data-cvalue.
*              iv_msgv3 =
*              iv_msgv4 = .

      ipaso = 0.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.
