*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_CANCELAR_UUID
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_cancelar_uuid
        USING p_bukrs
              p_numcomp
              p_uuid
              p_reason.
  DATA:
        lv_request_xml TYPE string.

  DATA:
    wa_sovos            TYPE zcp_sovos,
    wa_req_resp         TYPE zfit_filews_com,
    lv_version          TYPE zcfdide000,

    lv_sellocfd         TYPE zcfdide005,
    lv_sellosat         TYPE zcfdide006,
    lv_nocertificadosat TYPE zcfdide004,
    lv_cadena_org       TYPE string,
    lv_fechatimbradox   TYPE zcfdide003.

  DATA:
    p_rfcemix TYPE stcd1,
    p_rfcrecx TYPE zcfdide016.


  DATA: ws_timbrardes TYPE REF TO ztst40_co_icancelacion,
        st_input_t    TYPE ztst40_cancelacion_fiscal_requ,
        st_output_t   TYPE ztst40_cancelacion_fiscal_resp.
  DATA :
    lv_apikey TYPE zfe_apikey-apikey,
    e_error   TYPE string.
  DATA: ws_header TYPE REF TO if_wsprotocol_ws_header,
        name      TYPE string,
        namespace TYPE string,
        l_string  TYPE string,
        l_xstring TYPE xstring.
  DATA: ixml         TYPE REF TO if_ixml,
        xml_document TYPE REF TO if_ixml_document,
        xml_root     TYPE REF TO if_ixml_element,
        xml_element  TYPE REF TO if_ixml_element,
        xml_node     TYPE REF TO if_ixml_node.

  DATA: ws_timbrarpro TYPE REF TO zpro_co_iemision,
        st_input_p    TYPE zpro_emitir_comprobante_reques,
        ls_output     TYPE zpro_emitir_comprobante_respon.
  DATA: lcl_cx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.

  DATA: msg_error      TYPE string,
        p_sello_emisor TYPE string,
        p_sello_sat    TYPE string.
  DATA gv_rfcemisor     TYPE char13.

  "Estructuras de Complemento.

  DATA:
    it_cancel_folio TYPE zet_cancel_folio,
    st_cancel_folio TYPE zty_cancel_folio.

  CLEAR lv_apikey.

  DATA resstr TYPE string.
  DATA: st_property TYPE ztest_transaction_property.
  DATA:
    lv_buffer   TYPE xstring,
    gt_xml_data TYPE TABLE OF smum_xmltb,
    st_xml_data TYPE smum_xmltb,
    gt_return   TYPE TABLE OF bapiret2.

  "ZFE_APIKEY_ALL - Tabla con APikey.
  SELECT SINGLE apikey
    FROM zfe_apikey_all
    INTO lv_apikey
    WHERE bukrs = p_bukrs.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CONCATENATE TEXT-007 p_bukrs INTO e_error SEPARATED BY space.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

    IF sy-sysid EQ 'TPP'. "Sistema productivo
    ELSE.
      DATA v_ruta2   TYPE string.
      DATA param_dyn_value TYPE string.

      DATA:
        it_properties_t TYPE TABLE OF ztest_transaction_property,
        st_properties_t TYPE ztest_transaction_property.


*    st_input_t-controller = ''.            "RFC
*    st_input_t-transaction_properties = ''.
      SELECT SINGLE paval
      INTO gv_rfcemisor
      FROM t001z
      WHERE bukrs EQ p_bukrs
      AND party EQ 'MX_RFC'.

        st_cancel_folio-reason = p_reason.

        IF st_cancel_folio-reason = '01'.
*      st_cancel_folio-uuidsubstitute = uuid_cancel.
*      st_cancel_folio-uuid = gw_procesa-uuidp.
        ELSE.
          st_cancel_folio-uuid = p_uuid. "uuid_cancel.
        ENDIF.

        APPEND st_cancel_folio TO it_cancel_folio.

        st_input_t-folios-cancel_folio = it_cancel_folio .            "folios
        st_input_t-rfc                 = gv_rfcemisor.            "RFC

        st_properties_t-key    = 'TipoCFD'.
        st_properties_t-value  = 'P'. "'PAGO'.

        APPEND st_properties_t TO it_properties_t.
        st_input_t-transaction_properties-transaction_property = it_properties_t.


        TRY.
            CREATE OBJECT ws_timbrardes
              EXPORTING
                logical_port_name = 'ZTST40_CO_ICANCELACION_PORT'.

            ws_header ?= ws_timbrardes->get_protocol('IF_WSPROTOCOL_WS_HEADER').
*additional header:
            CONCATENATE
                        '<soapenv:Header>'
                        '<cus:ApiKey  xmlns:cus="http://www.reachcore.com/CFDI/customHeaders">'
*                      '5tvxwkbcyaxhsfr6jyhzpydskrm6ntwawnxgavxe5ztfakp7dj6v8hntimctz74nxq5rprwtvsxd6mex'   "Api Key MGS
                        lv_apikey
                        '</cus:ApiKey>'
                        '</soapenv:Header>'
                        ''

             INTO l_string.
*convert to xstring
            l_xstring = cl_proxy_service=>cstring2xstring( l_string ).

            IF NOT l_string IS INITIAL.
*create iXML DOM document from XML xstring
              CALL FUNCTION 'SDIXML_XML_TO_DOM'
                EXPORTING
                  xml           = l_xstring
                IMPORTING
                  document      = xml_document
                EXCEPTIONS
                  invalid_input = 1
                  OTHERS        = 2.
              IF sy-subrc = 0 AND NOT xml_document IS INITIAL.
                xml_root = xml_document->get_root_element( ).
                xml_element ?= xml_root->get_first_child( ).
*add header element by element to SOAP header
                WHILE NOT xml_element IS INITIAL.
                  name = xml_element->get_name( ).
                  namespace = xml_element->get_namespace_uri( ).
                  IF NOT xml_element IS INITIAL.
                  ENDIF.
                  ws_header->set_request_header(
                              name = name
                              namespace = namespace
                              dom = xml_element ).
                  xml_element ?= xml_element->get_next( ).
                ENDWHILE.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.



            CALL METHOD ws_timbrardes->cancelar
              EXPORTING
                cancelacion_fiscal_request  = st_input_t
              IMPORTING
                cancelacion_fiscal_response = st_output_t.
          CATCH cx_ai_system_fault INTO lcl_cx_root.
            msg_error = lcl_cx_root->get_text( ).
          CATCH cx_ai_application_fault INTO lcl_cx_root.
            msg_error = lcl_cx_root->get_text( ).
        ENDTRY.

        ls_output-error-inner_errors = st_output_t-error-inner_errors.

*    ls_output-result = st_output_t-result.

*    MOVE st_output_t-transaction_properties-transaction_property[] TO ls_output-transaction_properties-transaction_property[].

      ENDIF.

      DATA: lv_titulo TYPE char70,
            lv_linea1 TYPE char70,
            lv_linea2 TYPE char70.

      lv_titulo = 'Confirmación de Proceso'.
      lv_linea1 = |Éxito en la cancelación de CP: { p_numcomp }|.
      lv_linea2 = |tracking_id: { st_output_t-tracking_id }|.

      CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_DISPLAY_TEXT'
        EXPORTING
          titel        = lv_titulo
          textline1    = lv_linea1
          textline2    = lv_linea2
          start_column = 25
          start_row    = 6.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_PANTALLA_MOTIVOS_CANCEL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_IERROR  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_pantalla_motivos_cancel  CHANGING p_ierror.
  DATA: lt_fields TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF sval,
        ls_field  TYPE sval,
        lv_val1   TYPE string,
        lv_val2   TYPE string.

  " Primer campo
  CLEAR ls_field.
  ls_field-tabname    = 'DUMMY'.         " Obligatorio, aunque no se use
  ls_field-fieldname  = 'CAMPO1'.
  ls_field-fieldtext  = 'Categoría'.
  ls_field-field_obl  = 'X'.             " Campo obligatorio
  ls_field-value      = 'ALTA'.          " Valor por defecto
  APPEND ls_field TO lt_fields.

  " Segundo campo
  CLEAR ls_field.
  ls_field-tabname    = 'DUMMY'.
  ls_field-fieldname  = 'CAMPO2'.
  ls_field-fieldtext  = 'Prioridad'.
  ls_field-field_obl  = ' '.
  ls_field-value      = 'ALTA'.          " Valor por defecto
  APPEND ls_field TO lt_fields.

  " Mostrar popup
  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_GET_VALUES'
    EXPORTING
      popup_title     = 'Ingrese los valores'
    TABLES
      fields          = lt_fields
    EXCEPTIONS
      error_in_fields = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.

  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    LOOP AT lt_fields INTO ls_field.
      CASE ls_field-fieldname.
        WHEN 'CAMPO1'.
          lv_val1 = ls_field-value.
        WHEN 'CAMPO2'.
          lv_val2 = ls_field-value.
      ENDCASE.
    ENDLOOP.

    WRITE: / 'Valor CAMPO1:', lv_val1,
           / 'Valor CAMPO2:', lv_val2.
  ELSE.
    MESSAGE 'Popup cancelado o con error' TYPE 'I'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Persistir éxito de timbrado en ZTA1_CP1 (UPDATE + COMMIT) - clásico
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM z_persistir_exito_cp
  USING
    i_bukrs        TYPE zta1_cp1-bukrs
    i_ztpago       TYPE zta1_cp1-ztpago
    i_anio_pago    TYPE zta1_cp1-anio_pago
    i_ztfactrel    TYPE zta1_cp1-ztfactrel
    i_anio_fac     TYPE zta1_cp1-anio_fac
    i_numcomp      TYPE zta1_cp1-numcomp
    i_estatus      TYPE zta1_cp1-estatus
    i_text_est     TYPE zta1_cp1-text_est
    i_uuid         TYPE zta1_cp1-uuid_comp
    i_fechatimb    TYPE zta1_cp1-ztfetimb
    i_ruta_xml     TYPE zta1_cp1-ruta_xml
    i_ruta_pdf     TYPE zta1_cp1-ruta_pdf
    i_cert_pago    TYPE zta1_cp1-cert_pago
    i_xml_timb     TYPE zta1_cp1-xml_timb
  CHANGING
    c_subrc        TYPE sy-subrc
    c_msg          TYPE string.

  DATA lv_ts          TYPE timestampl.
  DATA lv_ts_c        TYPE char20.
  DATA lv_dbcnt_c     TYPE char10.
  DATA lv_subrc_c     TYPE char10.
  DATA lv_tmp         TYPE string.
  DATA lv_msg_text    TYPE string.

  c_subrc = 0.
  CLEAR: c_msg, lv_tmp, lv_ts_c, lv_dbcnt_c, lv_subrc_c, lv_msg_text.

  TRY.

      " === UPDATE con sintaxis clásica (sin @) ===
      UPDATE zta1_cp1
         SET estatus   = i_estatus
             text_est  = i_text_est
             uuid_comp = i_uuid
             ztfetimb  = i_fechatimb
             ruta_xml  = i_ruta_xml
             ruta_pdf  = i_ruta_pdf
             cert_pago = i_cert_pago
             xml_timb  = i_xml_timb
       WHERE bukrs     = i_bukrs
         AND ztpago    = i_ztpago
         AND anio_pago = i_anio_pago
         AND ztfactrel = i_ztfactrel
         AND anio_fac  = i_anio_fac
         AND numcomp   = i_numcomp.

      c_subrc = sy-subrc.
      GET TIME STAMP FIELD lv_ts.

      " Conversión numérico/temporal a texto para concatenar
      WRITE sy-dbcnt TO lv_dbcnt_c.
      WRITE lv_ts     TO lv_ts_c.
      WRITE c_subrc   TO lv_subrc_c.

      IF c_subrc = 0.
        COMMIT WORK AND WAIT.
        CONCATENATE 'ZTA1_CP1 actualizado (' lv_dbcnt_c 'fila/s) [' lv_ts_c '].'
               INTO lv_tmp SEPARATED BY space.
        c_msg = lv_tmp.
      ELSE.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        CONCATENATE 'No se pudo actualizar ZTA1_CP1 (sy-subrc=' lv_subrc_c ').'
               INTO lv_tmp.
        c_msg = lv_tmp.
      ENDIF.

    CATCH cx_sy_open_sql_db. " <-- sin objeto de excepción para evitar choques de test

      c_subrc = 4.
      WRITE c_subrc TO lv_subrc_c.
      ROLLBACK WORK.

      " Tomar el mensaje generado por el sistema (sy-msg*)
      CALL FUNCTION 'MESSAGE_TEXT_BUILD'
        EXPORTING
          msgid               = sy-msgid
          msgnr               = sy-msgno
          msgv1               = sy-msgv1
          msgv2               = sy-msgv2
          msgv3               = sy-msgv3
          msgv4               = sy-msgv4
        IMPORTING
          message_text_output = lv_msg_text.

      CONCATENATE 'Error SQL al actualizar ZTA1_CP1:' lv_msg_text
             INTO lv_tmp SEPARATED BY space.
      c_msg = lv_tmp.

  ENDTRY.

ENDFORM.
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Popup de resultado de timbrado (humano) - clásico
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM z_show_resultado_timbrado
  USING
    i_exito     TYPE abap_bool
    i_uuid      TYPE zta1_cp1-uuid_comp
    i_texto_pac TYPE string
    i_nom_xml   TYPE so_obj_des
    i_nom_pdf   TYPE so_obj_des.

  "--- Tipos y datos old school
  TYPES: ty_line TYPE c LENGTH 200.

  DATA: lt_lines  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_line,
        wa_line   TYPE ty_line,
        title_c70 TYPE c LENGTH 70.   " <--- C(70), NO STRING

  CLEAR: wa_line, title_c70.
  REFRESH lt_lines.

  "--- Título como C(70) (evitar literales string)
  IF i_exito = abap_true.
    title_c70 = 'Timbrado exitoso'.
  ELSE.
    title_c70 = 'Timbrado con errores'.
  ENDIF.

  "--- Armar líneas con CONCATENATE / APPEND
  IF i_exito = abap_true.
    IF i_uuid IS NOT INITIAL.
      CLEAR wa_line.
      CONCATENATE 'UUID:' i_uuid INTO wa_line SEPARATED BY space.
      APPEND wa_line TO lt_lines.
    ENDIF.

    IF i_nom_xml IS NOT INITIAL.
      CLEAR wa_line.
      CONCATENATE 'XML:' i_nom_xml INTO wa_line SEPARATED BY space.
      APPEND wa_line TO lt_lines.
    ENDIF.

    IF i_nom_pdf IS NOT INITIAL.
      CLEAR wa_line.
      CONCATENATE 'PDF:' i_nom_pdf INTO wa_line SEPARATED BY space.
      APPEND wa_line TO lt_lines.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF i_texto_pac IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR wa_line.
    CONCATENATE 'Mensaje PAC:' i_texto_pac INTO wa_line SEPARATED BY space.
    APPEND wa_line TO lt_lines.
  ENDIF.

  "--- Popup (TITLETEXT debe ser C(70))
  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_WITH_TABLE_DISPLAY'
    EXPORTING
      endpos_col   = 120
      endpos_row   = 25
      startpos_col = 5
      startpos_row = 5
      titletext    = title_c70
    TABLES
      value_tab    = lt_lines
    EXCEPTIONS
      OTHERS       = 1.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_EMITIR_BOTON
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Extrae datos del timbre CFDI (UUID, fecha, sellos, versión, RFCs)
* y actualiza/upsérta la tabla ZCP_SOVOS en un solo paso.
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Extrae timbre y hace upsert en ZCP_SOVOS (tipos CHANGING = string)  *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Extrae timbre del XML (xstring) y hace UPSERT en ZCP_SOVOS          *
* Todos los CHANGING de texto van con TYPE CLIKE para máxima compat.  *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_parse_and_upsert_sovos
  USING
    iv_xml_timb    TYPE xstring   " XML timbrado en xstring
    iv_bukrs       TYPE bukrs
    iv_numcomp     TYPE znumcomp
    iv_cad_orig    TYPE clike     " cadena original (opcional)
    iv_str_xml     TYPE clike     " XML como string (opcional)
  CHANGING
    cv_version       TYPE clike
    cv_sellosat      TYPE clike
    cv_sellocfd      TYPE clike
    cv_nocertsat     TYPE clike
    cv_rfcemi        TYPE clike
    cv_rfcrec        TYPE clike
    cv_fechatimbrado TYPE clike
    cv_uuid          TYPE clike.

  DATA: lt_xml         TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF smum_xmltb,
        lt_ret         TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapiret2,
        ls_xml         TYPE smum_xmltb,
        lv_is_emisor   TYPE abap_bool,
        lv_is_receptor TYPE abap_bool,
        wa_sovos       TYPE zcp_sovos.

  CLEAR: cv_version, cv_sellosat, cv_sellocfd, cv_nocertsat,
         cv_rfcemi,  cv_rfcrec,  cv_fechatimbrado, cv_uuid.

  IF iv_xml_timb IS INITIAL.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  " Parse directo del XSTRING a tabla SMUM_XMLTB
  CALL FUNCTION 'SMUM_XML_PARSE'
    EXPORTING
      xml_input = iv_xml_timb
    TABLES
      xml_table = lt_xml
      return    = lt_ret.

  " --- Atributos del Timbre Fiscal ---
  LOOP AT lt_xml INTO ls_xml.
    CASE ls_xml-cname.
      WHEN 'UUID'.              cv_uuid          = ls_xml-cvalue.
      WHEN 'Version'.           cv_version       = ls_xml-cvalue.
      WHEN 'Fecha'.             cv_fechatimbrado = ls_xml-cvalue.
      WHEN 'SelloCFD'.          cv_sellocfd      = ls_xml-cvalue.
      WHEN 'SelloSAT'.          cv_sellosat      = ls_xml-cvalue.
      WHEN 'NoCertificadoSAT'.  cv_nocertsat     = ls_xml-cvalue.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDLOOP.

  " --- RFC Emisor/Receptor ---
  CLEAR: lv_is_emisor, lv_is_receptor.
  LOOP AT lt_xml INTO ls_xml.
    CASE ls_xml-cname.
      WHEN 'Emisor'.    lv_is_emisor   = abap_true.  lv_is_receptor = abap_false.
      WHEN 'Receptor'.  lv_is_emisor   = abap_false. lv_is_receptor = abap_true.
      WHEN 'Rfc'.
        IF lv_is_emisor   = abap_true  AND cv_rfcemi IS INITIAL.
          cv_rfcemi = ls_xml-cvalue.
        ELSEIF lv_is_receptor = abap_true AND cv_rfcrec IS INITIAL.
          cv_rfcrec = ls_xml-cvalue.
        ENDIF.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDLOOP.

  " --- UPSERT en ZCP_SOVOS ---
  CLEAR wa_sovos.
  wa_sovos-bukrs            = iv_bukrs.
  wa_sovos-numcomp          = iv_numcomp.
  wa_sovos-fechatimbrado    = cv_fechatimbrado.
  wa_sovos-uuid             = cv_uuid.
  wa_sovos-version          = cv_version.
  wa_sovos-rfcemisor        = cv_rfcemi.
  wa_sovos-rfcreceptor      = cv_rfcrec.
  wa_sovos-sellocfd         = cv_sellocfd.
  wa_sovos-sellosat         = cv_sellosat.
  wa_sovos-cadena_original  = iv_cad_orig.
  wa_sovos-nocertificadosat = cv_nocertsat.
  wa_sovos-xml              = iv_xml_timb.
  wa_sovos-str_xml          = iv_str_xml.

  MODIFY zcp_sovos FROM wa_sovos.  "modifica por clave o inserta si no existe
ENDFORM.

*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Guardar y abrir el XML de error del PAC "como en éxito" (versión BIN)
* - Escribe siempre en BIN (UTF-8) para evitar parámetros ENCODING/CODEPAGE
*---------------------------------------------------------------------**---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Wrapper de compatibilidad:
* - Ajusta la llamada esperada F_OPEN_PAC_RESSTR_XML_SUCCESS
*   para que use F_OPEN_PAC_RESSTR_XML (firma distinta).
* - Si iv_numcomp viene vacío, intenta derivarlo de iv_name2
*   (p.ej. "CP0000000615.pdf" -> "0000000615").
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_open_pac_resstr_xml_success
  USING    iv_resstr   TYPE string
           iv_name2    TYPE string
           iv_numcomp  TYPE znumcomp
           iv_title    TYPE string.

  DATA: lv_bukrs   TYPE bukrs,
        lv_num_s   TYPE string,
        lv_num_eff TYPE znumcomp,
        lv_dotpos  TYPE i,
        lv_upper   TYPE string.

  CLEAR: lv_bukrs, lv_num_s, lv_num_eff.

  " 1) Determinar el número efectivo a usar
  IF iv_numcomp IS NOT INITIAL.
    lv_num_eff = iv_numcomp.
  ELSEIF iv_name2 IS NOT INITIAL.
    lv_num_s = iv_name2.

    " quitar extensión si trae (CP000...pdf -> CP000...)
    lv_dotpos = -1.
    FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF '.' IN lv_num_s MATCH OFFSET lv_dotpos.
    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND lv_dotpos > 0.
      lv_num_s = lv_num_s(lv_dotpos).
    ENDIF.

    " quitar prefijo CP si lo trae
    lv_upper = lv_num_s. TRANSLATE lv_upper TO UPPER CASE.
    IF strlen( lv_upper ) >= 2 AND lv_upper(2) = 'CP'.
      lv_num_s = lv_num_s+2.
    ENDIF.

    " asignar al tipo destino
    lv_num_eff = lv_num_s.
  ENDIF.

  " 2) Redirigir al FORM real (iv_bukrs no se usa para el nombre)
  PERFORM f_open_pac_resstr_xml
     USING iv_resstr
           lv_bukrs
           lv_num_eff
           iv_title.

ENDFORM.
